===== FILE: app\join\page.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';

type T = { code: string; registration_fee: number; renewal_fee: number };
type Designation = 'Imaging Technologist' | 'Radiographer' | 'Sonographer';
type JoinForm = {
  title: '' | 'Dr.' | 'Mr.' | 'Ms.';
  full_name: string;
  member_type: string;
  designation: '' | Designation;
  ahpc_no: string;
  reg_no: string;
  workplace: string;
  phone: string;
};

// Registration stays pending until Pesapal confirms payment.
export default function Join() {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState<string | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [already, setAlready] = useState(false);
  const [types, setTypes] = useState<T[]>([]);
  const [f, setF] = useState<JoinForm>({ title: '', full_name: '', member_type: 'professional', designation: '', ahpc_no: '', reg_no: '', workplace: '', phone: '' });
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    sb.auth.getUser().then(async ({ data }) => {
      setEmail(data.user?.email ?? null);
      if (!data.user) return;
      const { data: m } = await sb.from('members')
        .select('title,full_name,member_type,designation,ahpc_no,reg_no,workplace,phone,paid_through_year')
        .eq('id', data.user.id)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (m?.paid_through_year != null) return setAlready(true);
      if (m) {
        setF({
          title: m.title ?? '',
          full_name: m.full_name,
          member_type: m.member_type,
          designation: m.designation ?? '',
          ahpc_no: m.ahpc_no ?? '',
          reg_no: m.reg_no ?? '',
          workplace: m.workplace ?? '',
          phone: m.phone ?? '',
        });
        return;
      }
      const { data: a } = await sb.from('applications').select('title,full_name,member_type,designation,ahpc_no,reg_no,workplace,phone').eq('id', data.user.id).maybeSingle();
      if (a) {
        setF({
          title: a.title ?? '',
          full_name: a.full_name,
          member_type: a.member_type,
          designation: a.designation ?? '',
          ahpc_no: a.ahpc_no ?? '',
          reg_no: a.reg_no ?? '',
          workplace: a.workplace ?? '',
          phone: a.phone ?? '',
        });
      }
    });
    sb.from('membership_types').select('code,registration_fee,renewal_fee').then(({ data }) => setTypes(data ?? []));
  }, [sb]);

  const signIn = () => sb.auth.signInWithOAuth({
    provider: 'google',
    options: { redirectTo: `${location.origin}/auth/callback?next=/join`, queryParams: { prompt: 'select_account' } },
  });

  async function switchAccount() { await sb.auth.signOut(); location.reload(); }

  async function pay() {
    setBusy(true); setMsg('');
    const { data: { user } } = await sb.auth.getUser();
    if (!user) { setBusy(false); return; }
    if (!f.title) { setMsg('Choose a title.'); setBusy(false); return; }
    if (!f.full_name.trim()) { setMsg('Enter your full name.'); setBusy(false); return; }
    if (!f.designation) { setMsg('Choose your professional designation.'); setBusy(false); return; }

    const { error } = await sb.from('applications').upsert({
      id: user.id,
      email: user.email,
      ...f,
      ahpc_no: f.ahpc_no.trim() || null,
      reg_no: f.reg_no.trim() || null,
      workplace: f.workplace.trim() || null,
      phone: f.phone.trim() || null,
      full_name: f.full_name.trim(),
    });
    if (error) { console.error('Application save failed:', error); setMsg(`Could not save your details: ${error.message}`); setBusy(false); return; }

    try {
      const r = await fetch('/api/pay/start', { method: 'POST' });
      const j = await r.json();
      if (!r.ok || !j.redirect_url) {
        setMsg(j.error ?? 'Payment could not be started.');
        setBusy(false);
        return;
      }
      location.href = j.redirect_url;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Payment request failed:', error);
      setMsg('Could not connect to the payment service. Please try again.');
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const fee = types.find(t => t.code === f.member_type);
  const input = 'w-full rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:border focus:border-[#111318] focus:outline-none focus:ring-0';
  const label = 'mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700';
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-12">
      <WaveCard className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-gray-600">Society membership</p>
      <h1 className="mt-2 font-semibold text-2xl sm:text-3xl">Become a member</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-gray-600">Enter your professional and contact details to apply. Membership runs from 1 January to 31 December and begins only after payment is confirmed.</p>
      {email === undefined ? null : email === null ? (
        <button onClick={signIn} className="mt-6 rounded bg-teal-800 px-4 py-2 text-white">Continue with Google</button>
      ) : already ? (
        <p className="mt-6">You are already a member. <Link className="underline" href="/member">Go to my membership</Link> to renew or manage your records.</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
            Signed in as {email}.{' '}
            <button onClick={switchAccount} className="underline">Use a different Google account</button>
          </p>
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <label><span className={label}>Title</span>
              <select className={input} required value={f.title} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, title: e.target.value as JoinForm['title'] })}>
                <option value="">Select title</option><option value="Dr.">Dr.</option><option value="Mr.">Mr.</option><option value="Ms.">Ms.</option>
              </select>
            </label>
            <label><span className={label}>Full name</span>
              <input className={input} required autoComplete="name" placeholder="Enter your full name" value={f.full_name} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, full_name: e.target.value })} />
            </label>
            <label><span className={label}>Membership category</span>
              <select className={input} value={f.member_type} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, member_type: e.target.value })}>
                {types.map(type => <option key={type.code} value={type.code}>{type.code.replace(/[_-]/g, ' ')} member</option>)}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label><span className={label}>Designation</span>
              <select className={input} required value={f.designation} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, designation: e.target.value as JoinForm['designation'] })}>
                <option value="">Select designation</option>
                <option value="Imaging Technologist">Imaging Technologist</option>
                <option value="Radiographer">Radiographer</option>
                <option value="Sonographer">Sonographer</option>
              </select>
            </label>
            <label><span className={label}>AHPC No. <span className="font-normal text-gray-500">(optional)</span></span>
              <input className={input} autoComplete="off" placeholder="e.g. AHPC/12345" value={f.ahpc_no} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, ahpc_no: e.target.value })} />
            </label>
            <label><span className={label}>Workplace or school</span>
              <input className={input} autoComplete="organization" placeholder="Enter your hospital, clinic, or school" value={f.workplace} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, workplace: e.target.value })} />
            </label>
            <label><span className={label}>Phone number</span>
              <input className={input} type="tel" autoComplete="tel" placeholder="e.g. +256 7XX XXX XXX" value={f.phone} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, phone: e.target.value })} />
            </label>
            <label><span className={label}>Registration number <span className="font-normal text-gray-500">(if applicable)</span></span>
              <input className={input} placeholder="Enter your registration number" value={f.reg_no} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, reg_no: e.target.value })} />
            </label>
          </div>
          {fee && <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Registration fee: <span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">UGX {Number(fee.registration_fee).toLocaleString('en-US')}</span></p>}
          <button disabled={busy} onClick={pay} className="button-primary px-4 py-2.5 text-sm disabled:opacity-50">{busy ? 'Connecting to Pesapal...' : 'Continue to secure payment'}</button>
          {msg && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-700">{msg}</p>}
        </div>
      )}
      </WaveCard>
    </main>
  );
}


===== FILE: app\layout.tsx =====

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Inter } from 'next/font/google';
import './globals.css';
import GoogleOneTap from '@/components/GoogleOneTap';
import TopBar from '@/components/TopBar';
import Footer from '@/components/Footer';
import PublicMotionScope from '@/components/PublicMotionScope';

const inter = Inter({ subsets: ['latin'], variable: '--font-inter', display: 'swap' });

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Society of Radiography of Uganda',
  description: 'Advancing radiography practice in Uganda through professional development, standards, and a stronger professional community.',
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" className={inter.variable}>
      <body>
        <PublicMotionScope />
        <GoogleOneTap />
        <TopBar />
        {children}
        <Footer />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}



===== FILE: app\member\cv\page.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';

type Car = { id: string; kind: string; organisation: string; title: string; from_year: number; to_year: number | null; status: string };
type Cpd = { id: string; title: string; points: number; year: number };

export default function CvPage() {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const [year, setYear] = useState(0); // set after the page loads in the browser
  const [m, setM] = useState<any>(null);
  const [car, setCar] = useState<Car[]>([]);
  const [cpd, setCpd] = useState<Cpd[]>([]);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await sb.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) return setReady(true);
      const [a, b, c] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('members').select('full_name,email,phone,reg_no,member_type,workplace,bio').eq('id', user.id).maybeSingle(),
        sb.from('career_entries').select('id,kind,organisation,title,from_year,to_year,status').eq('member_id', user.id).order('from_year', { ascending: false }),
        sb.from('cpd_entries').select('id,title,points,year').eq('member_id', user.id).eq('status', 'verified').order('year', { ascending: false }),
      ]);
      setYear(new Date().getFullYear());
      setM(a.data); setCar(b.data ?? []); setCpd(c.data ?? []); setReady(true);
    })();
  }, [sb]);

  if (!ready) return <main className="p-8">Loading...</main>;
  if (!m) return <main className="p-8">Please <Link className="underline" href="/join">sign in</Link> first.</main>;

  const block = (kind: string) => car.filter(c => c.kind === kind).map(c => (
    <li key={c.id} className="py-2">
      <span className="text-gray-600">{c.from_year} to {c.to_year ?? 'present'}</span>{' '}
      <b>{c.title}</b>, {c.organisation}
      {c.status === 'verified' && <span className="ml-2 rounded border border-green-700 px-2 text-xs text-green-700">Verified by SRU</span>}
    </li>
  ));
  const total = cpd.reduce((a, c) => a + c.points, 0);
  const thisYear = cpd.filter(c => c.year === year).reduce((a, c) => a + c.points, 0);
  const h = 'mt-8 border-b border-gray-300 pb-1 text-lg font-semibold';

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl p-8">
      <div className="mb-6 flex gap-4 print:hidden">
        <button onClick={() => window.print()} className="rounded bg-teal-800 px-4 py-2 text-white">Print or save as PDF</button>
        <Link href="/member" className="py-2 underline">Back</Link>
      </div>
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold">{m.full_name}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-600">{m.email}{m.phone ? `, ${m.phone}` : ''}<br />SRU registration {m.reg_no ?? 'pending'}, {m.member_type} member</p>
      {m.bio && <p className="mt-4">{m.bio}</p>}
      <h2 className={h}>Employment</h2><ul className="divide-y divide-gray-200">{block('employment')}</ul>
      <h2 className={h}>Education</h2><ul className="divide-y divide-gray-200">{block('education')}</ul>
      <h2 className={h}>Continuing professional development</h2>
      <p className="mt-2">{total} verified points across all years, {thisYear} in {year}.</p>
      <ul className="divide-y divide-gray-200">{cpd.map(c => <li key={c.id} className="py-2"><span className="text-gray-600">{c.year}</span> {c.title}, {c.points} points</li>)}</ul>
    </main>
  );
}


===== FILE: app\member\page.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import ProfileSection from '@/components/member/ProfileSection';
import CpdSection from '@/components/member/CpdSection';
import CareerSection from '@/components/member/CareerSection';
import ArticlesSection from '@/components/member/ArticlesSection';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';

type Member = { id: string; full_name: string; member_type: string; paid_through_year: number | null };
type Type = { code: string; renewal_fee: number; late_fine: number; cpd_min: number };

const money = (n: number) => 'UGX ' + Number(n).toLocaleString('en-US');
const btn = 'button-primary px-4 py-2 disabled:opacity-50';
const memberPanels = [
  ['profile', 'Profile'],
  ['cpd', 'CPD'],
  ['career', 'Career'],
  ['articles', 'Articles'],
] as const;

export default function MemberPage() {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const [year, setYear] = useState(0); // set after the page loads in the browser
  const [state, setState] = useState<'loading' | 'signedout' | 'nomember' | 'ready'>('loading');
  const [hasApp, setHasApp] = useState(false);
  const [m, setM] = useState<Member | null>(null);
  const [t, setT] = useState<Type | null>(null);
  const [payMsg, setPayMsg] = useState('');
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState<'profile' | 'cpd' | 'career' | 'articles'>('profile');
  const tabRefs = useRef<Array<HTMLButtonElement | null>>([]);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data: { user } } = await sb.auth.getUser();
    if (!user) return setState('signedout');
    const { data: mem } = await sb.from('members').select('id,full_name,member_type,paid_through_year').eq('id', user.id).maybeSingle();
    if (!mem) {
      const { data: app } = await sb.from('applications').select('id').eq('id', user.id).maybeSingle();
      setHasApp(!!app);
      return setState('nomember');
    }
    const { data: ty } = await sb.from('membership_types').select('code,renewal_fee,late_fine,cpd_min').eq('code', mem.member_type).single();
    setYear(new Date().getFullYear());
    setM(mem); setT(ty); setState('ready');
  }, [sb]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  async function renew() {
    setPayMsg('');
    const r = await fetch('/api/pay/start', { method: 'POST' });
    const j = await r.json();
    if (j.redirect_url) location.href = j.redirect_url; else setPayMsg(j.error ?? 'Could not start payment.');
  }

  if (state === 'loading') return <main className="p-8">Loading...</main>;
  if (state === 'signedout') return <main className="mx-auto max-w-lg p-8"><p>Please sign in first.</p><Link className="underline" href="/join">Go to sign in</Link></main>;
  if (state === 'nomember') return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-lg p-8">
      <p>{hasApp ? 'Your registration is not complete. Your payment has not been confirmed yet.' : 'You are not a member yet.'}</p>
      <Link className="underline" href="/join">{hasApp ? 'Complete registration' : 'Become a member'}</Link>
    </main>
  );

  const active = (m!.paid_through_year ?? 0) >= year;

  return (
    <main className="workspace-compact mx-auto max-w-[1440px] space-y-3 px-3 py-4 sm:px-5">
      <WaveCard className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div>
            <h1 className="font-semibold">{m!.full_name}</h1>
            <p className="capitalize text-gray-600">{m!.member_type} member</p>
          </div>
          <Link href="/member/cv" className="rounded-md border border-gray-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50">Generate CV</Link>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 rounded-lg bg-gray-50 px-3 py-2">
          {active
            ? <p><b className="text-green-700">Active</b> until 31 December {m!.paid_through_year}.</p>
            : <p><b className="text-red-700">Expired</b> on 31 December {m!.paid_through_year}.</p>}
          {!active && t && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <p className="text-gray-600">Renewal {money(t.renewal_fee)}; any late fine is confirmed at checkout.</p>
              <button onClick={renew} className={btn}>Renew with Pesapal</button>
            </div>
          )}
          {active && <p className="text-gray-500">Renewal opens after 31 December.</p>}
          {payMsg && <p className="mt-2 text-red-700">{payMsg}</p>}
        </div>
      </WaveCard>

      <WaveCard className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm" index={1}>
      <section>
        <nav role="tablist" aria-label="Membership workspace" className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto border-b border-gray-200 p-1.5">
          {memberPanels.map(([key, label], index) => (
            <button
              key={key}
              ref={element => { tabRefs.current[index] = element; }}
              id={`member-tab-${key}`}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              tabIndex={panel === key ? 0 : -1}
              aria-selected={panel === key}
              aria-controls="member-panel"
              onClick={() => setPanel(key)}
              onKeyDown={event => {
                let next = index;
                if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') next = (index + 1) % memberPanels.length;
                else if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') next = (index - 1 + memberPanels.length) % memberPanels.length;
                else if (event.key === 'Home') next = 0;
                else if (event.key === 'End') next = memberPanels.length - 1;
                else return;
                event.preventDefault();
                setPanel(memberPanels[next][0]);
                tabRefs.current[next]?.focus();
              }}
              className="button-choice shrink-0 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs"
            >
              {label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div id="member-panel" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby={`member-tab-${panel}`} className="min-w-0 p-3 sm:p-4">
          {panel === 'profile' && <ProfileSection sb={sb} memberId={m!.id} />}
          {panel === 'cpd' && <CpdSection sb={sb} memberId={m!.id} min={t?.cpd_min ?? 0} />}
          {panel === 'career' && <CareerSection sb={sb} memberId={m!.id} />}
          {panel === 'articles' && <ArticlesSection sb={sb} memberId={m!.id} />}
        </div>
      </section>
      </WaveCard>
    </main>
  );
}



===== FILE: app\page.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import { Avatar, fmt, StatusBadge } from '@/components/ui';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import RevealSection from '@/components/RevealSection';
import TextReveal from '@/components/TextReveal';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';
import { EventRow, isUpcoming, feeLabel, safeUrl, localToday } from '@/lib/events';
import { useInView } from '@/lib/use-in-view';

type Slide = { id: number; headline: string; body: string | null; button_label: string | null; button_href: string | null; image_path: string | null };
type Art = { id: number; title: string; body: string; image_path: string | null; created_at: string; author_id: string | null };
type Showcase = { slot: number; label: string | null; sub_label: string | null; message: string; image_path: string | null };
type Ev = EventRow;
type Com = { term_id: number; starts_on: string; ends_on: string | null; is_current: boolean; role: string; full_name: string; photo_path: string | null; message: string | null };
type Prof = { id: string; full_name: string; avatar_path: string | null };
type Pub = (p: string) => string;

function CaptionLine() {
  const { ref, isVisible } = useInView<HTMLSpanElement>(0);

  return (
    <span
      ref={ref}
      aria-hidden="true"
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: 0,
        left: 0,
        width: '3rem',
        height: 2,
      }}
    >
      <span
        data-caption-line
        style={{
          display: 'block',
          width: '100%',
          height: '100%',
          backgroundColor: '#111318',
        transform: `scaleX(${isVisible ? 1 : 0})`,
        transformOrigin: 'left center',
        transition: 'transform 700ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)',
        }}
      />
    </span>
  );
}

function CountUp({ value }: { value: number }) {
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(0);
  const [inView, setInView] = useState(false);
  const countRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = countRef.current;
    if (!element) return;
    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      setInView(true);
      return;
    }

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      setInView(entry.isIntersecting);
    }, { threshold: 0.35 });
    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const target = Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(value)) : 0;
    if (!inView) {
      setShown(0);
      return;
    }
    if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
      setShown(target);
      return;
    }

    let frame = 0;
    let start: number | undefined;
    const duration = 1100;
    setShown(0);
    const animate = (now: number) => {
      if (start === undefined) start = now;
      const progress = Math.min(1, (now - start) / duration);
      const eased = 1 - (1 - progress) ** 3;
      setShown(Math.round(target * eased));
      if (progress < 1) frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [value, inView]);

  return <span ref={countRef} aria-label={String(value)}>{shown.toLocaleString('en-US')}</span>;
}

function ShowcaseBand({ item, pub, reverse }: { item: Showcase | undefined; pub: Pub; reverse: boolean }) {
  if (!item?.image_path || !item.message.trim()) return null;

  return (
    <RevealSection className="border-b border-gray-200 bg-white py-12 md:py-16">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-7xl items-center gap-8 px-6 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-14">
        <WaveCard index={reverse ? 1 : 0} className={`min-w-0 ${reverse ? 'lg:order-2' : 'lg:order-1'}`}>
          <figure>
            <img src={pub(item.image_path)} alt={item.label?.trim() || item.sub_label?.trim() || ''} className="h-56 w-full rounded-2xl object-cover shadow-md sm:h-80" />
            <figcaption className="mt-3">
              <span className="mb-2 inline-flex rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-gray-600">Ad</span>
              {item.label?.trim() && <TextReveal className="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold leading-tight text-gray-950 sm:text-3xl">{item.label.trim()}</TextReveal>}
              {item.sub_label?.trim() && <TextReveal delay={90} className="mt-1 text-sm font-normal text-gray-500 sm:text-base">{item.sub_label.trim()}</TextReveal>}
            </figcaption>
          </figure>
        </WaveCard>
        <WaveCard index={reverse ? 0 : 1} className={`min-w-0 ${reverse ? 'lg:order-1' : 'lg:order-2'}`}>
          <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-base leading-7 text-gray-700 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">{item.message}</p>
        </WaveCard>
      </div>
    </RevealSection>
  );
}

// Cycles through n items. `prev` lets the events panel slide the old one out as the new one comes in.
function useCycle(n: number, ms: number) {
  const [st, setSt] = useState({ cur: 0, prev: -1 });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (n < 2 || window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;
    const t = setInterval(() => setSt(s => ({ cur: (s.cur + 1) % n, prev: s.cur })), ms);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [n, ms]);
  return [st, setSt] as const;
}

function Hero({ slides, pub }: { slides: Slide[]; pub: Pub }) {
  const n = slides.length;
  const [st, setSt] = useCycle(n, 6000);
  const [zoomReady, setZoomReady] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => setZoomReady(true));
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, []);
  if (!n) return (
    <section className="bg-[radial-gradient(ellipse_at_top_right,_var(--tw-gradient-stops))] from-teal-800 via-slate-900 to-slate-950 py-24 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6">
        <p className="font-brand text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-teal-200">Society of Radiography of Uganda</p>
        <h1 className="mt-4 max-w-3xl font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-6xl"><TextReveal>Advancing imaging care. Together.</TextReveal></h1>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
  return (
    <section aria-label="Featured information" className="hero-entrance relative isolate min-h-[520px] overflow-hidden bg-slate-950 text-white">
      {slides.map((s, k) => (
        <div key={s.id} aria-hidden={k !== st.cur} className={`transition-opacity duration-700 ${k === st.cur ? 'relative opacity-100' : 'pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 opacity-0'}`}>
          {s.image_path && <img src={pub(s.image_path)} alt="" className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full scale-110 object-cover opacity-55 blur-xl" />}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-950/45" />
          <div className="relative mx-auto flex w-full max-w-7xl items-center px-5 py-14 sm:px-8 sm:py-16">
            <div className="grid w-full items-center gap-8 rounded-[1.5rem] border border-white bg-white p-5 text-gray-950 shadow-xl sm:gap-10 sm:p-8 lg:grid-cols-[0.9fr_1.1fr] lg:p-12">
              <div className="animate-rise-in max-w-2xl py-3">
                <p className="font-brand text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-gray-600 sm:hidden">Society of Radiography of Uganda</p>
                <h1 className="mt-4 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight text-gray-950 sm:text-5xl lg:text-6xl"><TextReveal>{s.headline}</TextReveal></h1>
                {s.body && <p className="mt-5 max-w-xl text-base leading-7 text-gray-700 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">{s.body}</p>}
                {s.button_label && <Link href={s.button_href || '/'} className="mt-7 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gray-950 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-white shadow-lg transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-black">{s.button_label}<span aria-hidden="true">â†’</span></Link>}
              </div>
              <div className="relative flex min-w-0 items-center justify-center">
                {s.image_path ? (
                  <div className="relative inline-flex max-w-full items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl">
                    <img src={pub(s.image_path)} alt="" className={`h-auto w-auto max-h-[min(460px,55vh)] max-w-full rounded-xl border border-gray-200 object-contain shadow-2xl transition-transform duration-[6000ms] ease-out ${zoomReady && k === st.cur ? 'scale-100' : 'scale-[1.055]'}`} />
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div aria-hidden="true" className="flex min-h-56 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">
                    <span className="font-brand text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-gray-500">SRU Â· Uganda</span>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
      {n > 1 && (
        <div className="absolute bottom-5 left-0 right-0 z-10">
          <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl gap-2 px-6 sm:px-8">
            {slides.map((s, k) => <button key={s.id} aria-label={`Go to slide ${k + 1}`} aria-current={k === st.cur} onClick={() => setSt({ cur: k, prev: st.cur })} className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all ${k === st.cur ? 'w-10 bg-white' : 'w-5 bg-white/45 hover:bg-white/75'}`} />)}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

// Latest articles, newest first, fading from one to the next.
function ArticleFade({ arts, profiles, pub }: { arts: Art[]; profiles: Record<string, Prof>; pub: Pub }) {
  const [st] = useCycle(arts.length, 6500);
  if (!arts.length) return <p className="text-gray-600">No articles yet.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      {arts.map((a, k) => {
        const p = a.author_id ? profiles[a.author_id] : null;
        const name = p?.full_name ?? 'SRU Secretariat';
        return (
          <article key={a.id} aria-hidden={k !== st.cur} inert={k !== st.cur} className={`bg-white p-4 transition-opacity duration-700 sm:p-5 ${k === st.cur ? 'relative opacity-100' : 'pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 opacity-0'}`}>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              {a.author_id
                ? <Link href={`/profile?id=${a.author_id}`} aria-label={`View ${name}'s profile`}><Avatar name={name} src={p?.avatar_path ? pub(p.avatar_path) : ''} size="h-9 w-9" /></Link>
                : <Avatar name={name} src="" size="h-9 w-9" />}
              <div>
                {a.author_id ? <Link href={`/profile?id=${a.author_id}`} className="text-sm font-medium leading-tight hover:underline">{name}</Link> : <div className="text-sm font-medium leading-tight">{name}</div>}
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">{fmt(a.created_at)}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            {a.image_path && <img src={pub(a.image_path)} alt="" className="mt-3 max-h-40 w-full rounded-lg object-cover sm:max-h-48" />}
            <h3 className="mt-3 font-serif text-lg font-semibold leading-snug sm:text-xl"><Link href={`/article?id=${a.id}`} className="hover:underline">{a.title}</Link></h3>
            <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-3 text-sm leading-6 text-gray-700">{a.body}</p>
            <Link href={`/article?id=${a.id}`} className="mt-2 inline-block text-sm font-medium text-teal-800 underline">Read the article</Link>
          </article>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

// Events: the current one slides up and out while the next one rises into place.
// Upcoming events show their fee and a Register Now button; past events show neither.
function EventRise({ evs, today, pub }: { evs: Ev[]; today: string; pub: Pub }) {
  const [st] = useCycle(evs.length, 5200);
  if (!evs.length) return <p className="text-gray-600">No events yet.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      {evs.map((e, k) => {
        const on = k === st.cur, out = k === st.prev;
        const up = isUpcoming(e, today), url = safeUrl(e.register_url);
        return (
          <article key={e.id}
            aria-hidden={!on}
            inert={!on}
            style={{ transform: on ? 'translateY(0)' : out ? 'translateY(-100%)' : 'translateY(100%)', transition: on || out ? 'transform 0.8s ease' : 'none' }}
            className={`bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 ${on ? 'relative' : 'pointer-events-none absolute inset-0'}`}>
            {e.image_path && <img src={pub(e.image_path)} alt="" className="max-h-36 w-full rounded-lg object-cover sm:max-h-40" />}
            <div className="mt-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500">{today && <StatusBadge upcoming={up} />}<span>{e.venue_date}</span></div>
            <h3 className="mt-1 font-serif text-lg font-semibold leading-snug sm:text-xl"><Link href={`/event?id=${e.id}`} className="hover:underline">{e.title}</Link></h3>
            {e.description && <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-3 text-sm leading-6 text-gray-700">{e.description}</p>}
            {up && (
              <div className="mt-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="rounded border border-gray-300 px-2 py-0.5 text-sm">{feeLabel(e)}</span>
                {url && <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="rounded-lg bg-teal-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-teal-900">Register now</a>}
              </div>
            )}
            <Link href={`/event?id=${e.id}`} className="mt-2 inline-block text-sm font-medium text-teal-800 underline">View event details</Link>
          </article>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

// Committee members one at a time. A committee with fromRight slides in from the right and out to the left;
// the other one slides in from the left and out to the right.
function CommitteeSlides({ rows, fromRight, ms, pub }: { rows: Com[]; fromRight: boolean; ms: number; pub: Pub }) {
  const [st, setSt] = useCycle(rows.length, ms);
  const wait = fromRight ? 'translateX(100%)' : 'translateX(-100%)';
  const exit = fromRight ? 'translateX(-100%)' : 'translateX(100%)';
  const ini = (n: string) => n.replace(/[^A-Za-z ]/g, ' ').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(-2).map(w => w[0]).join('').toUpperCase();
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="relative h-[500px] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm sm:h-[460px] md:h-[340px]">
        {rows.map((c, i) => {
          const on = i === st.cur, out = i === st.prev;
          return (
            <article key={i}
              style={{ transform: on ? 'translateX(0)' : out ? exit : wait, transition: on || out ? 'transform 0.8s ease' : 'none' }}
              className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col bg-white md:flex-row">
              {c.photo_path
                ? <div className="flex h-[220px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-gray-50 md:h-full md:w-[42%]"><img src={pub(c.photo_path)} alt={c.full_name} className="h-full w-full object-contain" /></div>
                : <div className="flex h-[220px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-900 to-slate-900 font-serif text-6xl text-white md:h-full md:w-[42%]">{ini(c.full_name)}</div>}
              <div className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col justify-center overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6 md:p-7">
                <h4 className="mt-2 font-serif text-xl font-semibold leading-tight sm:text-2xl">{c.full_name}</h4>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">{c.role}</p>
                {c.message && <p className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-line border-t border-gray-200 pt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-gray-600">{c.message}</p>}
              </div>
            </article>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      {rows.length > 1 && (
        <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
          {rows.map((_, i) => <button key={i} aria-label={`Member ${i + 1}`} onClick={() => setSt({ cur: i, prev: st.cur })} className={`h-1 w-8 ${i === st.cur ? 'bg-teal-800' : 'bg-gray-300'}`} />)}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Home() {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const pub: Pub = (p) => sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(p).data.publicUrl;
  const [slides, setSlides] = useState<Slide[]>([]);

  const [showcases, setShowcases] = useState<Showcase[]>([]);

  const [today, setToday] = useState('');
  const [com, setCom] = useState<Com[]>([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<{
    total: number;
    active: number;
    joined_this_year: number;
    imaging_technologists: number;
    sonographers: number;
    radiographers: number;
  } | null>(null);
  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<Record<string, Prof>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const [h, a, e, c, s, k, showcaseResult] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('hero_slides').select('*').eq('live', true).order('sort'),

        sb.rpc('public_committee'),

      setStats(s.data ?? null); setCfg(Object.fromEntries((k.data ?? []).map((r: any) => [r.key, r.value])));

    const past = evs.filter(e => !isUpcoming(e, today)).sort((a, b) => (b.ends_on ?? b.starts_on ?? '').localeCompare(a.ends_on ?? a.starts_on ?? ''));

  })();
  const h2 = 'font-serif text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl';

            <p className="font-brand text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-teal-800">Our purpose</p>

                  </div>

            <Link href="/committee" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 shadow-sm transition hover:border-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-900 hover:text-white">

===== FILE: app\payment\return\page.tsx =====

import { Suspense } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { settlePayment } from '@/lib/settle';

type SP = Promise<Record<string, string>>;

async function Result({ searchParams }: { searchParams: SP }) {
  const q = await searchParams;
  let state: 'completed' | 'pending' | 'failed' | 'unknown' = 'unknown';
  if (q.OrderTrackingId && q.OrderMerchantReference) {
    try {
      state = await settlePayment(q.OrderTrackingId, q.OrderMerchantReference);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Payment return verification failed:', error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error');
      state = 'pending';
    }
  }
  const text = {
    completed: ['Payment received', 'Your membership is now active. Thank you.'],
    pending: ['Payment is being confirmed', 'This can take a few minutes. Refresh this page shortly. You do not need to pay again.'],
    failed: ['Payment was not completed', 'You can try again.'],
    unknown: ['We could not find this payment', 'If you were charged, contact the Society with your payment receipt.'],
  }[state];
  return (
    <>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{text[0]}</h1>
      <p className="mt-3 text-gray-600">{text[1]}</p>
      <Link href={state === 'failed' ? '/join' : '/'} className="mt-6 inline-block rounded bg-teal-800 px-4 py-2 text-white">
        {state === 'failed' ? 'Try again' : 'Back to the home page'}
      </Link>
    </>
  );
}

// Members land here after paying on Pesapal. The page confirms the payment with Pesapal itself,
// so activation does not depend on the notification arriving first.
export default function PaymentReturn({ searchParams }: { searchParams: SP }) {
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-lg p-8">
      <Suspense fallback={<p>Confirming your payment...</p>}>
        <Result searchParams={searchParams} />
      </Suspense>
    </main>
  );
}

import { Suspense } from 'react';
import ProfileView from '@/components/ProfileView';

async function Inner({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<{ id?: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await searchParams;
  return <ProfileView id={id ?? ''} />;
}

export default function ProfilePage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<{ id?: string }> }) {
  return <Suspense fallback={<main className="p-8">Loading...</main>}><Inner searchParams={searchParams} /></Suspense>;
}



===== FILE: components\ArticleEngagement.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import { Avatar, fmt } from '@/components/ui';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

type Reaction = { member_id: string; kind: 'like' | 'unlike' };
type Cmt = { id: number; member_id: string; parent_id: number | null; body: string; created_at: string };
type Prof = { id: string; full_name: string; avatar_path: string | null };

const PAGE = 5;       // top-level comments shown before "Show more"
const MAX_INDENT = 3; // replies indent this many levels, then stay level so deep threads do not squeeze

const UP = 'M7 11v9H3v-9zM7 11l4-8c2 0 3 1 3 3v3h6l-1 8H7';
const DOWN = 'M7 13V4H3v9zM7 13l4 8c2 0 3-1 3-3v-3h6l-1-8H7';
const CHAT = 'M4 5h16v11H9l-5 4z';
const Icon = ({ d }: { d: string }) => (
  <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d={d} /></svg>
);
const Badge = ({ n }: { n: number }) => <span className="min-w-5 rounded-full bg-gray-200 px-1.5 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-900">{n}</span>;
const field = 'min-w-0 flex-1 rounded border border-gray-300 px-2 py-1.5 text-sm';
const btn = 'button-primary px-3 py-1.5 text-sm disabled:opacity-50';
const link = 'text-xs text-gray-600 hover:underline';

// Like and Unlike on the left; the full reply tree opens in a space-saving dialog.
export default function ArticleEngagement({ articleId }: { articleId: number }) {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const pub = (p: string) => sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(p).data.publicUrl;
  const [me, setMe] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [rx, setRx] = useState<Reaction[]>([]);
  const [cm, setCm] = useState<Cmt[]>([]);
  const [profs, setProfs] = useState<Record<string, Prof>>({});
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [reply, setReply] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [replyText, setReplyText] = useState('');
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [editText, setEditText] = useState('');
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [openNodes, setOpenNodes] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(PAGE);
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const closeRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        setOpen(false);
        return;
      }
      if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;
      const focusable = dialogRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
        'a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled])',
      );
      if (!focusable?.length) return;
      const first = focusable[0];
      const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    closeRef.current?.focus();
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      triggerRef.current?.focus();
    };
  }, [open]);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const [r, c] = await Promise.all([
      sb.from('article_reactions').select('member_id,kind').eq('article_id', articleId),
      sb.from('comments').select('id,member_id,parent_id,body,created_at').eq('article_id', articleId).order('created_at'),
    ]);
    if (r.error || c.error) {
      const error = r.error ?? c.error;
      console.error('Article discussion could not be loaded:', error);
      setFailed(true);
      setNote(`Could not load the discussion: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }
    setFailed(false);
    setRx((r.data as Reaction[]) ?? []); setCm((c.data as Cmt[]) ?? []);
    const ids = Array.from(new Set((c.data ?? []).map((x: Cmt) => x.member_id)));
    if (ids.length) {
      const { data, error } = await sb.from('public_profiles').select('id,full_name,avatar_path').in('id', ids);
      if (error) {
        console.error('Comment author profiles could not be loaded:', error);
        setNote(`Comments loaded, but author profiles could not be fetched: ${error.message}`);
        return;
      }
      setProfs(Object.fromEntries((data ?? []).map((p: Prof) => [p.id, p])));
    } else {
      setProfs({});
    }
    setNote('');
  }, [sb, articleId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await sb.auth.getUser();
      if (user) {
        const { data: m } = await sb.from('members').select('paid_through_year').eq('id', user.id).maybeSingle();
        setMe(m?.paid_through_year != null ? user.id : null);
        const { data: role } = await sb.rpc('my_role');
        setIsAdmin(role === 'chief_admin' || role === 'admin');
      }
      await load();
      setReady(true);
    })();
  }, [sb, load]);

  // Keep the thread stable if legacy comments refer to a missing parent or contain a cycle.
  const kids = useMemo(() => {
    const ids = new Set(cm.map(c => c.id));
    const parents = new Map<number, number | null>();
    cm.forEach(comment => {
      parents.set(comment.id, comment.parent_id && comment.parent_id !== comment.id && ids.has(comment.parent_id)
        ? comment.parent_id
        : null);
    });

    const complete = new Set<number>();
    for (const comment of cm) {
      const path: number[] = [];
      const positions = new Map<number, number>();
      let current: number | null = comment.id;
      while (current !== null && !complete.has(current)) {
        const cycleStart = positions.get(current);
        if (cycleStart !== undefined) {
          const root = Math.min(...path.slice(cycleStart));
          parents.set(root, null);
          break;
        }
        positions.set(current, path.length);
        path.push(current);
        current = parents.get(current) ?? null;
      }
      path.forEach(id => complete.add(id));
    }

    const map = new Map<number, Cmt[]>();
    cm.forEach(c => {
      const key = parents.get(c.id) ?? 0;
      map.set(key, [...(map.get(key) ?? []), c]);
    });
    return map;
  }, [cm]);
  const countBelow = (id: number): number => {
    const seen = new Set<number>();
    const pending = [...(kids.get(id) ?? [])];
    while (pending.length) {
      const current = pending.pop()!;
      if (seen.has(current.id)) continue;
      seen.add(current.id);
      pending.push(...(kids.get(current.id) ?? []));
    }
    return seen.size;
  };
  const tops = kids.get(0) ?? [];

  const mine = rx.find(x => x.member_id === me)?.kind;
  const likes = rx.filter(x => x.kind === 'like').length;
  const unlikes = rx.filter(x => x.kind === 'unlike').length;

  async function react(kind: 'like' | 'unlike') {
    if (!me) return setNote('Only members can like or comment.');
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const { error } = mine === kind
      ? await sb.from('article_reactions').delete().eq('article_id', articleId).eq('member_id', me)
      : await sb.from('article_reactions').upsert({ article_id: articleId, member_id: me, kind }, { onConflict: 'article_id,member_id' });
    setBusy(false);
    setNote(error ? 'Could not save your reaction. ' + error.message : '');
    load();
  }

  async function post(parent: number | null) {
    if (!me) return setNote('Only members can like or comment.');
    const body = (parent ? replyText : text).trim();
    if (!body) return setNote('Write something first.');
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const { error } = await sb.from('comments').insert({ article_id: articleId, member_id: me, parent_id: parent, body });
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setNote('Could not post. ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    setNote('');
    if (parent) { setReply(null); setReplyText(''); setOpenNodes(s => new Set(s).add(parent)); } else setText('');
    load();
  }
  async function saveEdit(id: number) {
    const body = editText.trim();
    if (!body || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const { error } = await sb.from('comments').update({ body }).eq('id', id);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setNote('Could not save. ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    setEditId(null); load();
  }
  async function del(id: number) {
    const n = countBelow(id);
    if (!confirm(n ? `Delete this comment and its ${n} repl${n === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies'}?` : 'Delete this comment?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('comments').delete().eq('id', id);
    if (error) setNote('Could not delete. ' + error.message);
    else playUiSound('delete');
    load();
  }
  const toggle = (id: number) => setOpenNodes(s => { const n = new Set(s); n.has(id) ? n.delete(id) : n.add(id); return n; });
  const cancelOn = (fn: () => void) => (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') fn(); };

  // One comment, then (when expanded) its replies. A plain function, so typing never loses focus.
  const node = (c: Cmt, depth: number): React.ReactNode => {
    const p = profs[c.member_id];
    const name = p?.full_name ?? 'Member';
    const children = kids.get(c.id) ?? [];
    const expanded = openNodes.has(c.id);
    return (
      <div style={{ marginLeft: Math.min(depth, MAX_INDENT) * 14 }} className={'py-2 ' + (depth ? 'border-l border-gray-200 pl-3' : '')}>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Link href={`/profile?id=${c.member_id}`} aria-label={`View ${name}'s profile`} className="shrink-0"><Avatar name={name} src={p?.avatar_path ? pub(p.avatar_path) : ''} size="h-7 w-7" /></Link>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-2 text-xs">
                <Link href={`/profile?id=${c.member_id}`} className="text-sm font-medium hover:underline">{name}</Link>
                <span className="text-gray-500">{fmt(c.created_at)}</span>
                {me && <button className={link} onClick={() => { setReply(reply === c.id ? null : c.id); setReplyText(''); }}>Reply</button>}
                {me === c.member_id && <button className={link} onClick={() => { setEditId(c.id); setEditText(c.body); }}>Edit</button>}
                {(me === c.member_id || isAdmin) && <button className={link} onClick={() => del(c.id)}>Delete</button>}
              </div>
              {editId === c.id ? (
                <div className="mt-1 flex gap-2">
                  <input className={field} autoFocus value={editText} maxLength={2000} onChange={e => setEditText(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') saveEdit(c.id); else cancelOn(() => setEditId(null))(e); }} />
                  <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={() => saveEdit(c.id)}>Save</button>
                  <button className={link} onClick={() => setEditId(null)}>Cancel</button>
                </div>
              ) : <p className="whitespace-pre-line break-words text-sm text-gray-800">{c.body}</p>}
              {reply === c.id && (
                <div className="mt-1 flex gap-2">
                  <input className={field} autoFocus placeholder={`Reply to ${name}`} value={replyText} maxLength={2000} onChange={e => setReplyText(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') post(c.id); else cancelOn(() => setReply(null))(e); }} />
                  <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={() => post(c.id)}>Reply</button>
                  <button className={link} onClick={() => setReply(null)}>Cancel</button>
                </div>
              )}
              {children.length > 0 && (
                <button className={link + ' mt-0.5'} aria-expanded={expanded} onClick={() => toggle(c.id)}>
                  {expanded ? 'Hide replies' : `View ${countBelow(c.id)} ${countBelow(c.id) === 1 ? 'reply' : 'replies'}`}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
    );
  };

  const composer = () => me ? (
    <div className="sticky bottom-0 flex gap-2 border-t border-gray-200 bg-white py-2">
      <input ref={inputRef} className={field} placeholder="Add a comment" value={text} maxLength={2000} onChange={e => setText(e.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') post(null); }} />
      <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={() => post(null)}>Post</button>
    </div>
  ) : <p className="pt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">Only members can comment. <Link className="underline" href="/join">Become a member</Link></p>;

  const thread = () => {
    const visible: Array<{ comment: Cmt; depth: number }> = [];
    const pending = tops.slice(0, shown).reverse().map(comment => ({ comment, depth: 0 }));
    while (pending.length) {
      const current = pending.pop()!;
      visible.push(current);
      if (openNodes.has(current.comment.id)) {
        const children = kids.get(current.comment.id) ?? [];
        for (let i = children.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          pending.push({ comment: children[i], depth: current.depth + 1 });
        }
      }
    }

    return (
      <div>
        <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
          {visible.map(({ comment, depth }) => <div key={comment.id}>{node(comment, depth)}</div>)}
          {tops.length === 0 && !failed && <p className="py-2 text-sm text-gray-500">No comments yet.</p>}
        </div>
        {tops.length > shown && <button className={link + ' mt-1'} onClick={() => setShown(s => s + PAGE)}>Show {Math.min(PAGE, tops.length - shown)} more comments</button>}
        {failed && <p className="py-2 text-sm text-red-700">Could not load comments. <button className="underline" onClick={load}>Try again</button></p>}
        {composer()}
      </div>
    );
  };

  const pill = 'gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 text-sm disabled:opacity-60';
  return (
    <section className="mt-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-y border-gray-200 py-2">
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => react('like')} aria-pressed={mine === 'like'} className={`button-success ${pill}`}><Icon d={UP} />Like<Badge n={likes} /></button>
          <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => react('unlike')} aria-pressed={mine === 'unlike'} className={`button-danger ${pill}`}><Icon d={DOWN} />Unlike<Badge n={unlikes} /></button>
        </div>
        <button ref={triggerRef} onClick={() => setOpen(true)} className={`button-choice ${pill}`}><Icon d={CHAT} />Comments<Badge n={cm.length} /></button>
      </div>
      {ready && !me && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">Only members can like or comment. <Link className="underline" href="/join">Become a member</Link></p>}
      {note && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-700">{note}</p>}

      {open && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/55 p-3 backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-6"
          onMouseDown={event => {
            if (event.target === event.currentTarget) setOpen(false);
          }}
        >
          <div
            ref={dialogRef}
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="article-comments-title"
            className="flex max-h-[88vh] w-full max-w-3xl animate-rise-in flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-2xl"
          >
            <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-gray-200 px-4 py-3 sm:px-5">
              <div>
                <h3 id="article-comments-title" className="font-serif text-base font-semibold">Comments</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{cm.length} {cm.length === 1 ? 'comment' : 'comments'}</p>
              </div>
              <button ref={closeRef} type="button" className="button-neutral px-3 py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Close</button>
            </header>
            <div className="overflow-y-auto px-4 py-2 sm:px-5">
              {thread()}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\ArticleView.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import { Avatar, fmt } from '@/components/ui';
import ArticleEngagement from '@/components/ArticleEngagement';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';
import TextReveal from '@/components/TextReveal';

export default function ArticleView({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const pub = (p: string) => sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(p).data.publicUrl;
  const [a, setA] = useState<any>(null);
  const [p, setP] = useState<any>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const { data } = await sb.from('articles').select('id,title,body,image_path,created_at,author_id,status').eq('id', id).maybeSingle();
      setA(data);
      if (data?.author_id) {
        const { data: pr } = await sb.from('public_profiles').select('full_name,avatar_path').eq('id', data.author_id).maybeSingle();
        setP(pr);
      }
      setDone(true);
    })();
  }, [sb, id]);

  if (!done) return <main className="p-8">Loading...</main>;
  if (!a) return <main className="p-8">Article not found. <Link className="underline" href="/">Home</Link></main>;
  const name = p?.full_name ?? 'SRU Secretariat';
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-6 py-10">
      <Link href="/#articles" className="text-sm underline">Back to articles</Link>
      <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        {a.author_id
          ? <Link href={`/profile?id=${a.author_id}`} aria-label={`View ${name}'s profile`}><Avatar name={name} src={p?.avatar_path ? pub(p.avatar_path) : ''} /></Link>
          : <Avatar name={name} src="" />}
        <div>
          {a.author_id ? <Link href={`/profile?id=${a.author_id}`} className="font-medium leading-tight hover:underline">{name}</Link> : <div className="font-medium leading-tight">{name}</div>}
          <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">{fmt(a.created_at)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <WaveCard className="mt-6">
        {a.image_path && <img src={pub(a.image_path)} alt="" className="w-full object-cover" />}
        <h1 className="mt-6 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold"><TextReveal>{a.title}</TextReveal></h1>
        <div className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-line text-lg leading-relaxed text-gray-800">{a.body}</div>
        {a.status === 'approved' && <ArticleEngagement articleId={a.id} />}
      </WaveCard>
    </main>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\CommitteeDirectory.tsx =====

'use client';

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import RevealSection from '@/components/RevealSection';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';

type CommitteeMember = {
  member_id: number | null;
  term_id: number;
  starts_on: string;
  ends_on: string | null;
  is_current: boolean;
  role: string | null;
  full_name: string | null;
  photo_path: string | null;
  message: string | null;
};

function initials(name: string) {
  return name.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(-2).map(part => part[0] ?? '').join('').toUpperCase();
}

function formatTerm(start: string, end: string | null) {
  const startYear = start.slice(0, 4);
  const endYear = end?.slice(0, 4);
  return endYear ? `${startYear} â€“ ${endYear}` : `${startYear} â€“ Present`;
}

export default function CommitteeDirectory() {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const [members, setMembers] = useState<CommitteeMember[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    (async () => {
      const { data, error: queryError } = await sb.rpc('public_all_committees');
      if (!active) return;
      if (queryError) {
        console.error('Committee directory could not be loaded:', queryError);
        setError(queryError.code === 'PGRST202'
          ? 'The committee directory is not installed yet. Run supabase/patch-13-public-committee-directory.sql in the Supabase SQL Editor, then reload this page.'
          : `Could not load the committee directory: ${queryError.message}`);
      } else {
        setMembers((data ?? []) as CommitteeMember[]);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    })();

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [sb]);

  const groups = useMemo(() => {
    const byTerm = new Map<number, CommitteeMember[]>();
    for (const member of members) {
      const group = byTerm.get(member.term_id) ?? [];
      group.push(member);
      byTerm.set(member.term_id, group);
    }
    return Array.from(byTerm.entries()).map(([termId, rows]) => ({ termId, rows }));
  }, [members]);
  const photoUrl = (path: string) => sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl;
  const currentGroup = groups.find(group => group.rows[0]?.is_current);
  const pastGroups = groups.filter(group => !group.rows[0]?.is_current);

  function renderTerm({ termId, rows }: typeof groups[number], groupIndex: number, label?: string) {
    const first = rows[0];
    const roster = rows.filter(member => member.member_id !== null);
    return (
      <section key={termId} aria-labelledby={`committee-term-${termId}`}>
        <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            {label && <p className="font-brand text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-teal-800">{label}</p>}
            <h2 id={`committee-term-${termId}`} className={`${label ? 'mt-1' : ''} font-serif text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl`}>{formatTerm(first.starts_on, first.ends_on)}</h2>
          </div>
          <span className="rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-white px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-gray-600">{roster.length} {roster.length === 1 ? 'member' : 'members'}</span>
        </div>
        {roster.length === 0
          ? <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-300 bg-white p-5 text-sm text-gray-600">No members listed for this term.</p>
          : <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {roster.map((member, index) => (
              <WaveCard key={member.member_id} index={groupIndex * 3 + index} className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
                <div className="flex h-56 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-gray-100">
                  {member.photo_path
                    ? <img src={photoUrl(member.photo_path)} alt={member.full_name ?? ''} className="h-full w-full object-contain" />
                    : <span aria-hidden="true" className="font-serif text-5xl font-semibold text-gray-400">{initials(member.full_name ?? '')}</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="p-5">
                  {member.role && <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600">{member.role}</p>}
                  <h3 className="mt-1 font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-gray-950">{member.full_name}</h3>
                </div>
              </WaveCard>
            ))}
          </div>}
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main>
      <RevealSection className="border-b border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 py-12 md:py-16">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6">
          <Link href="/#committee" className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-950">â† Back to homepage</Link>
          <p className="mt-6 font-brand text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-teal-800">Leadership</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">Our Committees</h1>
          <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-gray-600">Meet the current and past committee members serving the Society of Radiography of Uganda.</p>
        </div>
      </RevealSection>

      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-12 px-6 py-10 md:py-14">
        {loading && <p role="status" className="text-gray-600">Loading committee membersâ€¦</p>}
        {!loading && error && <p role="alert" className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-800">{error}</p>}
        {!loading && !error && groups.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-600">No committee members have been added yet.</p>}
        {!loading && !error && currentGroup && renderTerm(currentGroup, 0, 'Current committee')}
        {!loading && !error && pastGroups.length > 0 && (
          <>
            {currentGroup && <hr className="border-0 border-t border-gray-200" />}
            <section aria-labelledby="past-committees-title" className="space-y-8">
              <h2 id="past-committees-title" className="font-brand text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-teal-800">Past committees</h2>
              {pastGroups.map((group, index) => renderTerm(group, index + (currentGroup ? 1 : 0)))}
            </section>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\ArticlesTab.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, editBtn, successBtn, dangerBtn, pub, uploadPublic, removePublic } from './shared';
import PhotoField from './PhotoField';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

type Art = { id: number; title: string; body: string; image_path: string | null; status: string; created_at: string; author_id: string | null; decline_reason: string | null; author_name: string | null };
const LABEL: Record<string, [string, string]> = { approved: ['Published', 'text-green-700'], pending: ['Awaiting approval', 'text-amber-700'], declined: ['Declined', 'text-red-700'] };

export default function ArticlesTab({ sb }: { sb: SB }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Art[]>([]);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');
  const [why, setWhy] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [f, setF] = useState({ title: '', body: '' });
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [remove, setRemove] = useState(false);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data, error } = await sb.from('articles')
      .select('id,title,body,image_path,status,created_at,author_id,decline_reason')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    if (error) {
      console.error('Articles load failed:', error);
      setMsg(`Could not load articles: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }

    const authorIds = Array.from(new Set((data ?? []).map(article => article.author_id).filter((id): id is string => !!id)));
    let names = new Map<string, string>();
    if (authorIds.length) {
      const { data: members, error: membersError } = await sb.from('members').select('id,full_name').in('id', authorIds);
      if (membersError) {
        console.error('Article author lookup failed:', membersError);
        setMsg(`Articles loaded, but author names could not be fetched: ${membersError.message}`);
      } else {
        names = new Map((members ?? []).map(member => [member.id, member.full_name]));
        setMsg('');
      }
    } else {
      setMsg('');
    }
    setRows((data ?? []).map(article => ({
      ...article,
      author_name: article.author_id ? names.get(article.author_id) ?? null : null,
    })));
  }, [sb]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const editing = editId ? rows.find(r => r.id === editId) : undefined;
  const reset = () => { setEditId(null); setF({ title: '', body: '' }); setPhoto(null); setRemove(false); setRk(k => k + 1); };
  const author = (a: Art) => a.author_id ? a.author_name ?? 'Member author' : 'SRU Secretariat';

  function edit(a: Art) {
    setEditId(a.id); setF({ title: a.title, body: a.body }); setPhoto(null); setRemove(false); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('');
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  }

  async function setStatus(a: Art, status: string, reason: string | null = null) {
    const { error } = await sb.from('articles').update({ status, decline_reason: reason }).eq('id', a.id);
    if (!error) playUiSound('success');
    setMsg(error ? 'Could not update: ' + error.message : '');
    load();
  }
  function decline(a: Art) {
    const reason = (why[a.id] ?? '').trim();
    if (!reason) return setMsg('Write a reason before declining, so the author knows what to change.');
    setStatus(a, 'declined', reason);
  }

  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!f.title.trim() || !f.body.trim()) return setMsg('Enter a title and the article text.');
    setBusy(true);
    const old = editing?.image_path ?? null;
    let image_path: string | null | undefined;
    if (photo) {
      const r = await uploadPublic(sb, 'articles', photo);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The photo could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      image_path = r.path!;
    } else if (remove) image_path = null;

    const payload: any = { title: f.title.trim(), body: f.body.trim() };
    if (image_path !== undefined) payload.image_path = image_path;
    if (!editId) Object.assign(payload, { author_id: null, status: 'approved' });   // posted as the Secretariat

    const { error } = editId ? await sb.from('articles').update(payload).eq('id', editId) : await sb.from('articles').insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save: ' + error.message);
    if (editId && image_path !== undefined) await removePublic(sb, old);
    playUiSound('success');
    setMsg(editId ? 'Article updated.' : 'Article published.'); reset(); load();
  }

  async function del(a: Art) {
    if (!confirm('Delete this article?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('articles').delete().eq('id', a.id);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not delete: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('delete');
    await removePublic(sb, a.image_path); if (editId === a.id) reset(); load();
  }

  const count = (s: string) => rows.filter(r => r.status === s).length;
  const shown = rows.filter(r => filter === 'all' || r.status === filter);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Member articles are shown to visitors only after you approve them. Articles you post here appear as the Secretariat and are published straight away.</p>
      {msg && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-700">{msg}</p>}

      <div className="space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-medium">{editId ? 'Edit article' : 'Post an article as the Secretariat'}</h2>
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} placeholder="Title" value={f.title} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, title: e.target.value })} />
        <textarea className={input + ' w-full'} rows={8} placeholder="Article text" value={f.body} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, body: e.target.value })} />
        <PhotoField sb={sb} current={editing?.image_path ?? null} file={photo} setFile={setPhoto} remove={remove} setRemove={setRemove} resetKey={rk} />
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : editId ? 'Save changes' : 'Publish'}</button>
          {editId && <button className={alt} onClick={reset}>Cancel</button>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
        {[['all', `All (${rows.length})`], ['pending', `Awaiting approval (${count('pending')})`], ['approved', `Published (${count('approved')})`], ['declined', `Declined (${count('declined')})`]].map(([k, l]) => (
          <button key={k} aria-pressed={filter === k} onClick={() => setFilter(k)} className="button-choice px-3 py-1 text-sm">{l}</button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <ul className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
        {shown.map(a => {
          const [label, cls] = LABEL[a.status] ?? [a.status, ''];
          return (
            <li key={a.id} className="py-3">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                {a.image_path ? <img src={pub(sb, a.image_path)} alt="" className="h-14 w-24 object-cover" /> : <div className="h-14 w-24 bg-gray-100" />}
                <div className="min-w-48 flex-1 text-sm"><b>{a.title}</b><div className="text-gray-500">{author(a)} Â· <span className={cls}>{label}</span></div></div>
                {a.status === 'approved' && <button className={alt} onClick={() => setStatus(a, 'pending')}>Unpublish</button>}
                <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => edit(a)}>Edit</button>
                <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => del(a)}>Delete</button>
              </div>
              {a.status === 'pending' && (
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <button className={successBtn} onClick={() => setStatus(a, 'approved')}>Approve</button>
                  <input className={input + ' w-64'} placeholder="Reason for declining" value={why[a.id] ?? ''} onChange={e => setWhy({ ...why, [a.id]: e.target.value })} />
                  <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => decline(a)}>Decline</button>
                </div>
              )}
              {a.decline_reason && <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">Reason: {a.decline_reason}</div>}
            </li>
          );
        })}
        {shown.length === 0 && <li className="py-3 text-sm text-gray-500">Nothing here.</li>}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\CommitteeTab.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, editBtn, dangerBtn, pub, uploadPublic, removePublic } from './shared';
import PhotoField from './PhotoField';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

type Term = { id: number; starts_on: string; ends_on: string | null };
type Mem = { id: number; term_id: number; role: string; full_name: string; email: string; phone: string | null; workplace: string | null; photo_path: string | null; message: string | null; sort: number };
const blank = { term: '', role: '', full_name: '', email: '', phone: '', workplace: '', message: '' };

export default function CommitteeTab({ sb }: { sb: SB }) {
  const [terms, setTerms] = useState<Term[]>([]);
  const [mems, setMems] = useState<Mem[]>([]);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [newStart, setNewStart] = useState('');
  const [f, setF] = useState(blank);
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [removePhoto, setRemovePhoto] = useState(false);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [editTerm, setEditTerm] = useState<Term | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const [t, m] = await Promise.all([
      sb.from('committee_terms').select('*').order('starts_on', { ascending: false }),
      sb.from('committee').select('*').order('sort').order('id'),
    ]);
    setTerms(t.data ?? []); setMems(m.data ?? []);
    setF(x => x.term ? x : { ...x, term: String((t.data ?? []).find((r: Term) => !r.ends_on)?.id ?? (t.data ?? [])[0]?.id ?? '') });
  }, [sb]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const current = editId ? mems.find(m => m.id === editId)?.photo_path ?? null : null;
  const resetForm = () => { setEditId(null); setF(x => ({ ...blank, term: x.term })); setPhoto(null); setRemovePhoto(false); setRk(k => k + 1); };

  // ---- Terms ----
  async function startTerm() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!newStart) return setMsg('Choose the date the new term starts.');
    if (!confirm('Start a new term on ' + newStart + '? The current committee will become the previous committee.')) return;
    const { data, error } = await sb.rpc('start_new_term', { p_start: newStart });
    if (error) return setMsg(error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    setNewStart(''); setF(x => ({ ...x, term: String(data) })); setMsg('New term started. Add its members below.'); load();
  }
  async function saveTerm() {
    if (!editTerm) return;
    if (!editTerm.starts_on) return setMsg('A term needs a start date.');
    if (editTerm.ends_on && editTerm.ends_on < editTerm.starts_on) return setMsg('The end date cannot be before the start date.');
    const { error } = await sb.from('committee_terms').update({ starts_on: editTerm.starts_on, ends_on: editTerm.ends_on || null }).eq('id', editTerm.id);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save the term: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    setEditTerm(null); setMsg('Term updated.'); load();
  }
  async function deleteTerm(t: Term) {
    const list = mems.filter(m => m.term_id === t.id);
    if (!confirm(`Delete the term ${t.starts_on} to ${t.ends_on ?? 'present'} and its ${list.length} member(s)? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
    await Promise.all(list.map(m => removePublic(sb, m.photo_path)));
    const { error } = await sb.from('committee_terms').delete().eq('id', t.id);
    if (!error) playUiSound('delete');
    setMsg(error ? 'Could not delete the term: ' + error.message : 'Term deleted.'); load();
  }

  // ---- Members ----
  function edit(m: Mem) {
    setEditId(m.id); setPhoto(null); setRemovePhoto(false); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('');
    setF({ term: String(m.term_id), role: m.role, full_name: m.full_name, email: m.email, phone: m.phone ?? '', workplace: m.workplace ?? '', message: m.message ?? '' });
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  }
  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!f.term) return setMsg('Choose a term.');
    if (!f.role.trim() || !f.full_name.trim()) return setMsg('Enter the role and the full name.');
    if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(f.email)) return setMsg('Enter the memberâ€™s Google account email.');
    setBusy(true);
    const old = current;
    let photo_path: string | null | undefined;           // undefined means unchanged
    if (photo) {
      const r = await uploadPublic(sb, 'committee', photo);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The photo could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      photo_path = r.path!;
    } else if (removePhoto) photo_path = null;

    const payload: any = {
      term_id: Number(f.term), role: f.role.trim(), full_name: f.full_name.trim(), email: f.email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      phone: f.phone.trim() || null, workplace: f.workplace.trim() || null, message: f.message.trim() || null,
    };
