    if (photo_path !== undefined) payload.photo_path = photo_path;
    if (!editId) payload.sort = mems.filter(m => m.term_id === Number(f.term)).length;

    const { error } = editId ? await sb.from('committee').update(payload).eq('id', editId) : await sb.from('committee').insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    if (editId && photo_path !== undefined) await removePublic(sb, old);   // clear the replaced photo
    setMsg(editId ? 'Member updated.' : 'Member added.'); resetForm(); load();
  }
  async function remove(m: Mem) {
    if (!confirm('Remove ' + m.full_name + ' from the committee?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('committee').delete().eq('id', m.id);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not remove: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('delete');
    await removePublic(sb, m.photo_path); if (editId === m.id) resetForm(); load();
  }
  async function move(termId: number, id: number, d: number) {
    const list = mems.filter(m => m.term_id === termId);
    const i = list.findIndex(m => m.id === id), j = i + d;
    if (j < 0 || j >= list.length) return;
    [list[i], list[j]] = [list[j], list[i]];
    const results = await Promise.all(list.map((m, k) => sb.from('committee').update({ sort: k }).eq('id', m.id)));
    const failed = results.find(result => result.error)?.error;
    if (failed) return setMsg('Could not reorder committee members: ' + failed.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    load();
  }

  const ini = (n: string) => n.replace(/[^A-Za-z ]/g, ' ').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(-2).map(w => w[0]).join('').toUpperCase();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Each committee serves a period. The current committee is the term with no end date. Visitors see the current committee and the most recent previous one. Every term stays here.</p>
      {msg && <p className="text-sm text-gray-800">{msg}</p>}

      <div className="rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-medium">Start a new term</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">The current term ends the day before the new one starts.</p>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input type="date" className={input} value={newStart} onChange={e => setNewStart(e.target.value)} aria-label="New term start date" />
          <button className={btn} onClick={startTerm}>Start new term</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-medium">{editId ? 'Edit committee member' : 'Add a committee member'}</h2>
        <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <select className={input} value={f.term} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, term: e.target.value })}>
              {terms.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.starts_on} to {t.ends_on ?? 'present'}</option>)}
            </select>
            <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Role, e.g. President" value={f.role} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, role: e.target.value })} />
            <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Full name" value={f.full_name} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, full_name: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <input className={input + ' flex-1'} type="email" placeholder="Google account email" value={f.email} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, email: e.target.value })} />
            <input className={input} placeholder="Phone" value={f.phone} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, phone: e.target.value })} />
            <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Workplace and qualification" value={f.workplace} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, workplace: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">
            Message for the public committee card
            <textarea className={input + ' mt-1 w-full'} rows={3} maxLength={500} placeholder="A short message about your work or vision for the profession" value={f.message} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, message: e.target.value })} />
            <span className="mt-1 block text-right font-normal text-gray-500">{f.message.length}/500</span>
          </label>
          <PhotoField sb={sb} current={current} file={photo} setFile={setPhoto} remove={removePhoto} setRemove={setRemovePhoto} resetKey={rk} round />
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : editId ? 'Save changes' : 'Add member'}</button>
            {editId && <button className={alt} onClick={resetForm}>Cancel</button>}
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Email and phone are kept private. Visitors see the portrait, name, role and message.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {terms.map(t => {
        const list = mems.filter(m => m.term_id === t.id);
        return (
          <div key={t.id}>
            {editTerm?.id === t.id ? (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <label>Starts <input type="date" className={input} value={editTerm.starts_on} onChange={e => setEditTerm({ ...editTerm, starts_on: e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>Ends <input type="date" className={input} value={editTerm.ends_on ?? ''} onChange={e => setEditTerm({ ...editTerm, ends_on: e.target.value || null })} /></label>
                <button className={btn} onClick={saveTerm}>Save term</button>
                <button className={alt} onClick={() => setEditTerm(null)}>Cancel</button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-semibold">{t.starts_on} to {t.ends_on ?? 'present'}</h2>
                <span className="rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-gray-600">{list.length} {list.length === 1 ? 'member' : 'members'}</span>
                {!t.ends_on && <span className="rounded border border-green-700 px-2 text-xs text-green-700">Current</span>}
                <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => setEditTerm(t)}>Edit dates</button>
                <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => deleteTerm(t)}>Delete term</button>
              </div>
            )}
            <ul className="mt-2 divide-y divide-gray-200">
              {list.map((m, i) => (
                <li key={m.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 py-2">
                  {m.photo_path
                    ? <img src={pub(sb, m.photo_path)} alt="" className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full object-cover" />
                    : <span className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-800 text-sm font-semibold text-white">{ini(m.full_name)}</span>}
                  <div className="min-w-48 flex-1 text-sm"><b>{m.full_name}</b>, {m.role}<div className="text-gray-500">{m.email}{m.phone ? `, ${m.phone}` : ''}</div>{m.message && <div className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-gray-600">{m.message}</div>}</div>
                  <button className={alt} disabled={i === 0} onClick={() => move(t.id, m.id, -1)}>Up</button>
                  <button className={alt} disabled={i === list.length - 1} onClick={() => move(t.id, m.id, 1)}>Down</button>
                  <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => edit(m)}>Edit</button>
                  <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => remove(m)}>Remove</button>
                </li>
              ))}
              {list.length === 0 && <li className="py-2 text-sm text-gray-500">No members yet.</li>}
            </ul>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\EventsTab.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, editBtn, dangerBtn, pub, uploadPublic, removePublic } from './shared';
import PhotoField from './PhotoField';
import { EventRow, isUpcoming, feeLabel, safeUrl, localToday } from '@/lib/events';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

const blank = { title: '', venue_date: '', starts_on: '', ends_on: '', description: '', register_url: '', paid: 'free', fee_amount: '' };

export default function EventsTab({ sb }: { sb: SB }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<EventRow[]>([]);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [f, setF] = useState(blank);
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [removePhoto, setRemovePhoto] = useState(false);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [today, setToday] = useState('');

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await sb.from('events').select('*').order('id', { ascending: false });
    setRows(data ?? []); setToday(localToday());
  }, [sb]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const current = editId ? rows.find(r => r.id === editId)?.image_path ?? null : null;
  const reset = () => { setEditId(null); setF(blank); setPhoto(null); setRemovePhoto(false); setRk(k => k + 1); };

  function edit(e: EventRow) {
    setEditId(e.id); setPhoto(null); setRemovePhoto(false); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('');
    setF({
      title: e.title, venue_date: e.venue_date ?? '', starts_on: e.starts_on ?? '', ends_on: e.ends_on ?? '', description: e.description ?? '',
      register_url: e.register_url ?? '', paid: e.is_paid ? 'paid' : 'free', fee_amount: e.fee_amount != null ? String(e.fee_amount) : '',
    });
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  }

  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!f.title.trim()) return setMsg('Enter the event title.');
    if (!f.starts_on) return setMsg('Choose the start date. It decides when the event counts as previous.');
    if (f.ends_on && f.ends_on < f.starts_on) return setMsg('The end date cannot be before the start date.');
    if (f.register_url.trim() && !safeUrl(f.register_url.trim())) return setMsg('The registration link must start with http:// or https://');
    const paid = f.paid === 'paid', fee = Number(f.fee_amount);
    if (paid && (!fee || fee <= 0)) return setMsg('Enter the fee amount for a paid event.');

    setBusy(true);
    const old = current;
    let image_path: string | null | undefined;
    if (photo) {
      const r = await uploadPublic(sb, 'events', photo);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The photo could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      image_path = r.path!;
    } else if (removePhoto) image_path = null;

    const payload: any = {
      title: f.title.trim(), venue_date: f.venue_date.trim() || null, starts_on: f.starts_on, ends_on: f.ends_on || null,
      description: f.description.trim() || null, register_url: f.register_url.trim() || null, is_paid: paid, fee_amount: paid ? fee : null,
    };
    if (image_path !== undefined) payload.image_path = image_path;

    const { error } = editId ? await sb.from('events').update(payload).eq('id', editId) : await sb.from('events').insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save the event: ' + error.message);
    if (editId && image_path !== undefined) await removePublic(sb, old);
    playUiSound('success');
    setMsg(editId ? 'Event updated.' : 'Event added.'); reset(); load();
  }

  async function remove(e: EventRow) {
    if (!confirm('Delete this event?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('events').delete().eq('id', e.id);
    if (error) return setMsg(error.message);
    playUiSound('delete');
    await removePublic(sb, e.image_path); if (editId === e.id) reset(); load();
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Upcoming events show their fee and a Register Now button. Once an eventâ€™s last day has passed, the button and the fee are hidden automatically.</p>
      {msg && <p className="text-sm">{msg}</p>}

      <div className="space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-medium">{editId ? 'Edit event' : 'Add an event'}</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Event title" value={f.title} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, title: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Venue and dates, as shown to visitors" value={f.venue_date} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, venue_date: e.target.value })} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <label>Starts <input type="date" className={input} value={f.starts_on} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, starts_on: e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label>Ends (if more than one day) <input type="date" className={input} value={f.ends_on} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, ends_on: e.target.value })} /></label>
        </div>
        <textarea className={input + ' w-full'} rows={4} placeholder="Description" value={f.description} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, description: e.target.value })} />
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} placeholder="Registration link, e.g. https://..." value={f.register_url} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, register_url: e.target.value })} />
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <select className={input} value={f.paid} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, paid: e.target.value })} aria-label="Free or paid">
            <option value="free">Free</option><option value="paid">Paid</option>
          </select>
          {f.paid === 'paid' && <input className={input + ' w-44'} type="number" min="1" placeholder="Amount in UGX" value={f.fee_amount} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, fee_amount: e.target.value })} />}
        </div>
        <PhotoField sb={sb} current={current} file={photo} setFile={setPhoto} remove={removePhoto} setRemove={setRemovePhoto} resetKey={rk} />
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : editId ? 'Save changes' : 'Add event'}</button>
          {editId && <button className={alt} onClick={reset}>Cancel</button>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <ul className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
        {rows.map(e => {
          const up = isUpcoming(e, today);
          return (
            <li key={e.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 py-3">
              {e.image_path ? <img src={pub(sb, e.image_path)} alt="" className="h-14 w-24 object-cover" /> : <div className="h-14 w-24 bg-gray-100" />}
              <div className="min-w-48 flex-1 text-sm">
                <b>{e.title}</b>
                <div className="text-gray-500">{e.venue_date}</div>
                <div className="text-gray-500">{e.starts_on ?? 'No date'}{e.ends_on ? ` to ${e.ends_on}` : ''} Â· {feeLabel(e)}{e.register_url ? ' Â· Link set' : ''}</div>
              </div>
              <span className={'text-sm ' + (up ? 'text-green-700' : 'text-gray-500')}>{today ? (up ? 'Upcoming' : 'Previous') : ''}</span>
              <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => edit(e)}>Edit</button>
              <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => remove(e)}>Delete</button>
            </li>
          );
        })}
        {rows.length === 0 && <li className="py-3 text-sm text-gray-500">No events yet.</li>}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\HeroTab.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, editBtn, dangerBtn, pub, uploadPublic, removePublic } from './shared';
import PhotoField from './PhotoField';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

type Slide = { id: number; headline: string; body: string | null; button_label: string | null; button_href: string | null; image_path: string | null; sort: number; live: boolean };
const blank = { headline: '', body: '', button_label: '', button_href: '' };

export default function HeroTab({ sb }: { sb: SB }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Slide[]>([]);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [f, setF] = useState(blank);
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [removePhoto, setRemovePhoto] = useState(false);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await sb.from('hero_slides').select('*').order('sort').order('id');
    setRows(data ?? []);
  }, [sb]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const current = editId ? rows.find(r => r.id === editId)?.image_path ?? null : null;
  const reset = () => { setEditId(null); setF(blank); setPhoto(null); setRemovePhoto(false); setRk(k => k + 1); };

  function edit(s: Slide) {
    setEditId(s.id); setPhoto(null); setRemovePhoto(false); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('');
    setF({ headline: s.headline, body: s.body ?? '', button_label: s.button_label ?? '', button_href: s.button_href ?? '' });
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  }

  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!f.headline.trim()) return setMsg('Enter a headline.');
    setBusy(true);
    const old = current;
    let image_path: string | null | undefined;
    if (photo) {
      const r = await uploadPublic(sb, 'hero', photo);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The photo could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      image_path = r.path!;
    } else if (removePhoto) image_path = null;

    const payload: any = {
      headline: f.headline.trim(), body: f.body.trim() || null,
      button_label: f.button_label.trim() || null, button_href: f.button_href.trim() || null,
    };
    if (image_path !== undefined) payload.image_path = image_path;
    if (!editId) { payload.sort = rows.length + 1; payload.live = true; }

    const { error } = editId ? await sb.from('hero_slides').update(payload).eq('id', editId) : await sb.from('hero_slides').insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save the slide: ' + error.message);
    if (editId && image_path !== undefined) await removePublic(sb, old);
    playUiSound('success');
    setMsg(editId ? 'Slide updated.' : 'Slide added.'); reset(); load();
  }

  async function toggle(s: Slide) {
    const { error } = await sb.from('hero_slides').update({ live: !s.live }).eq('id', s.id);
    if (error) setMsg(error.message);
    else playUiSound('success');
    load();
  }
  async function move(i: number, d: number) {
    const j = i + d;
    if (j < 0 || j >= rows.length) return;
    const order = rows.slice(); [order[i], order[j]] = [order[j], order[i]];
    const results = await Promise.all(order.map((s, k) => sb.from('hero_slides').update({ sort: k + 1 }).eq('id', s.id)));
    const failed = results.find(result => result.error)?.error;
    if (failed) return setMsg('Could not reorder the slides: ' + failed.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    load();
  }
  async function remove(s: Slide) {
    if (!confirm('Delete this slide?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('hero_slides').delete().eq('id', s.id);
    if (error) return setMsg(error.message);
    playUiSound('delete');
    await removePublic(sb, s.image_path); if (editId === s.id) reset(); load();
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Slides appear on the landing page in this order. Hidden slides stay here but are not shown to visitors.</p>
      {msg && <p className="text-sm">{msg}</p>}

      <div className="space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-medium">{editId ? 'Edit slide' : 'Add a slide'}</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Headline" value={f.headline} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, headline: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Short text" value={f.body} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, body: e.target.value })} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Button label" value={f.button_label} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, button_label: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Button link, e.g. /join" value={f.button_href} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, button_href: e.target.value })} />
        </div>
        <PhotoField sb={sb} current={current} file={photo} setFile={setPhoto} remove={removePhoto} setRemove={setRemovePhoto} resetKey={rk} />
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : editId ? 'Save changes' : 'Add slide'}</button>
          {editId && <button className={alt} onClick={reset}>Cancel</button>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <ul className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
        {rows.map((s, i) => (
          <li key={s.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 py-3">
            {s.image_path ? <img src={pub(sb, s.image_path)} alt="" className="h-14 w-24 object-cover" /> : <div className="h-14 w-24 bg-gray-100" />}
            <div className="min-w-48 flex-1 text-sm"><b>{s.headline}</b><div className="text-gray-500">{s.button_label ? `Button: ${s.button_label}` : 'No button'}</div></div>
            <span className={'text-sm ' + (s.live ? 'text-green-700' : 'text-amber-700')}>{s.live ? 'Live' : 'Hidden'}</span>
            <button className="button-choice px-3 py-1.5" aria-pressed={s.live} onClick={() => toggle(s)}>{s.live ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}</button>
            <button className={alt} disabled={i === 0} onClick={() => move(i, -1)}>Up</button>
            <button className={alt} disabled={i === rows.length - 1} onClick={() => move(i, 1)}>Down</button>
            <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => edit(s)}>Edit</button>
            <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => remove(s)}>Delete</button>
          </li>
        ))}
        {rows.length === 0 && <li className="py-3 text-sm text-gray-500">No slides yet.</li>}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\PhotoField.tsx =====

'use client';
import { SB, pub } from './shared';

// Photo control for an add or edit form: shows the current photo, lets you replace it, or remove it.
export default function PhotoField({ sb, current, file, setFile, remove, setRemove, resetKey, round }: {
  sb: SB; current: string | null; file: File | null; setFile: (f: File | null) => void;
  remove: boolean; setRemove: (b: boolean) => void; resetKey: number; round?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-sm">
      {current && !remove && !file && <img src={pub(sb, current)} alt="Current photo" className={`h-14 w-14 object-cover ${round ? 'rounded-full' : ''}`} />}
      <label>{current ? 'Replace photo' : 'Photo'}{' '}
        <input key={resetKey} type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={e => { const f = e.target.files?.[0] ?? null; setFile(f); if (f) setRemove(false); }} />
      </label>
      {current && !file && (
        <label className="flex items-center gap-1"><input type="checkbox" checked={remove} onChange={e => setRemove(e.target.checked)} /> Remove current photo</label>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\PurposeTab.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn } from './shared';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

const defaults = {
  purpose_headline: 'A stronger voice for radiography in Uganda.',
  purpose_body: 'SRU brings radiographers together to advance professional standards, support lifelong learning, and strengthen the quality of imaging care.',
  purpose_card_1_title: 'Professional community',
  purpose_card_1_body: 'Connect with radiographers and contribute to the profession.',
  purpose_card_2_title: 'Continuing development',
  purpose_card_2_body: 'Record verified CPD and follow your annual progress.',
  purpose_card_3_title: 'Career recognition',
  purpose_card_3_body: 'Build a trusted record of education and work experience.',
};

type PurposeContent = typeof defaults;

export default function PurposeTab({ sb }: { sb: SB }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState<PurposeContent>(defaults);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data, error } = await sb.from('site_settings').select('key,value');
    if (error) {
      console.error('Purpose content could not be loaded:', error);
      setMessage(`Could not load purpose content: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }
    const values = Object.fromEntries((data ?? []).map(row => [row.key, row.value])) as Partial<PurposeContent>;
    setForm(Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(defaults).map(([key, fallback]) => [key, values[key as keyof PurposeContent] || fallback]),
    ) as PurposeContent);
    setMessage('');
  }, [sb]);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  async function save() {
    setMessage('');
    setBusy(true);
    const { error } = await sb.from('site_settings').upsert(
      Object.entries(form).map(([key, value]) => ({ key, value: value.trim() })),
    );
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) {
      console.error('Purpose content could not be saved:', error);
      setMessage(`Could not save purpose content: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }
    playUiSound('success');
    setMessage('Purpose content saved.');
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Edit the Our Purpose section and its three feature cards on the public landing page.</p>
      {message && <p role="status" className="text-sm text-gray-700">{message}</p>}
      <div className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h3 className="font-medium">Purpose statement</h3>
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} aria-label="Purpose headline" value={form.purpose_headline} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, purpose_headline: event.target.value })} />
        <textarea className={input + ' w-full'} rows={3} aria-label="Purpose description" value={form.purpose_body} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, purpose_body: event.target.value })} />
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
        {[1, 2, 3].map(index => {
          const titleKey = `purpose_card_${index}_title` as keyof PurposeContent;
          const bodyKey = `purpose_card_${index}_body` as keyof PurposeContent;
          return (
            <section key={index} className="space-y-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-4">
              <h3 className="font-medium">Feature {index}</h3>
              <input className={input + ' w-full'} aria-label={`Feature ${index} title`} value={form[titleKey]} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, [titleKey]: event.target.value })} />
              <textarea className={input + ' w-full'} rows={3} aria-label={`Feature ${index} description`} value={form[bodyKey]} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, [bodyKey]: event.target.value })} />
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : 'Save purpose content'}</button>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\SettingsTab.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, dangerBtn, pub, uploadPublic, removePublic } from './shared';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

// Contact details shown on the landing page, and the Society's logo shown in the top bar.
export default function SettingsTab({ sb }: { sb: SB }) {
  const [c, setC] = useState({ contact_email: '', contact_phone: '', contact_address: '' });
  const [logo, setLogo] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await sb.from('site_settings').select('key,value');
    const m: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries((data ?? []).map((r: any) => [r.key, r.value]));
    setC({ contact_email: m.contact_email ?? '', contact_phone: m.contact_phone ?? '', contact_address: m.contact_address ?? '' });
    setLogo(m.logo_path ?? '');
  }, [sb]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  async function saveContacts() {
    setMsg('');
    const { error } = await sb.from('site_settings').upsert(Object.entries(c).map(([key, value]) => ({ key, value: value.trim() })));
    if (!error) playUiSound('success');
    setMsg(error ? 'Could not save: ' + error.message : 'Contacts saved.');
  }
  async function saveLogo() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!file) return setMsg('Choose a logo image first.');
    setBusy(true);
    const old = logo;
    const r = await uploadPublic(sb, 'branding', file);
    if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The logo could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
    const { error } = await sb.from('site_settings').upsert({ key: 'logo_path', value: r.path! });
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save the logo: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    await removePublic(sb, old);
    setFile(null); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('Logo saved. Reload the page to see it in the top bar.'); load();
  }
  async function removeLogo() {
    if (!logo || !confirm('Remove the logo from the site?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('site_settings').delete().eq('key', 'logo_path');
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not remove the logo: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('delete');
    await removePublic(sb, logo); setMsg('Logo removed.'); load();
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      {msg && <p className="text-sm">{msg}</p>}
      <div className="space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-medium">Contacts</h2>
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} placeholder="Email" value={c.contact_email} onChange={e => setC({ ...c, contact_email: e.target.value })} />
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} placeholder="Phone" value={c.contact_phone} onChange={e => setC({ ...c, contact_phone: e.target.value })} />
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} placeholder="Address" value={c.contact_address} onChange={e => setC({ ...c, contact_address: e.target.value })} />
        <button className={btn} onClick={saveContacts}>Save contacts</button>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h2 className="font-medium">Logo</h2>
        {logo ? <img src={pub(sb, logo)} alt="Current logo" className="h-16" /> : <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">No logo uploaded yet.</p>}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <input key={rk} type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} />
          <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={saveLogo}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : logo ? 'Replace logo' : 'Save logo'}</button>
          {logo && <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={removeLogo}>Remove logo</button>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\content\shared.ts =====

import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';

export type SB = ReturnType<typeof browser>;
export const input = 'rounded border border-gray-300 p-2 focus:border focus:border-[#111318] focus:outline-none focus:ring-0';
export const btn = 'button-primary px-3 py-1.5 disabled:opacity-50';
export const alt = 'button-neutral px-3 py-1.5 disabled:opacity-50';
export const editBtn = 'button-edit px-3 py-1.5 disabled:opacity-50';
export const successBtn = 'button-success px-3 py-1.5 disabled:opacity-50';
export const dangerBtn = 'button-danger px-3 py-1.5 disabled:opacity-50';
export const pub = (sb: SB, p: string) => sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(p).data.publicUrl;

// Uploads a photo to the public media bucket. Returns the stored path, or an error message.
export async function uploadPublic(sb: SB, folder: string, file: File): Promise<{ path?: string; error?: string }> {
  const path = `${folder}/${Date.now()}-${file.name.replace(/[^\w.\-]/g, '_')}`;
  const { error } = await sb.storage.from('public-media').upload(path, file);
  return error ? { error: error.message } : { path };
}

// Deletes a stored photo. Used when a photo is replaced or removed, or its record is deleted.
export async function removePublic(sb: SB, path: string | null | undefined) {
  if (path) await sb.storage.from('public-media').remove([path]);
}



===== FILE: components\content\ShowcasesTab.tsx =====

'use client';

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, dangerBtn, pub, uploadPublic } from './shared';
import PhotoField from './PhotoField';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

type Slot = 1 | 2 | 3;
type Showcase = { slot: Slot; label: string | null; sub_label: string | null; message: string; image_path: string | null };
type Form = { label: string; subLabel: string; message: string; photo: File | null; removePhoto: boolean; resetKey: number };

const slots: Slot[] = [1, 2, 3];
const emptyForm = (): Form => ({ label: '', subLabel: '', message: '', photo: null, removePhoto: false, resetKey: 0 });

export default function ShowcasesTab({ sb }: { sb: SB }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Showcase[]>([]);
  const [forms, setForms] = useState<Record<Slot, Form>>({ 1: emptyForm(), 2: emptyForm(), 3: emptyForm() });
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [busySlot, setBusySlot] = useState<Slot | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data, error } = await sb.from('homepage_showcases').select('slot,label,sub_label,message,image_path').order('slot');
    if (error) {
      console.error('Homepage showcases could not be loaded:', error);
      const migrationMissing = error.code === '42P01' || error.code === 'PGRST205';
      const subLabelMissing = error.code === '42703' || error.code === 'PGRST204';
      setMessage(migrationMissing
        ? 'The homepage showcases table is not installed. Run supabase/patch-11-homepage-showcases.sql in the Supabase SQL Editor, then reload this page.'
        : subLabelMissing
          ? 'The showcase sub-label field is not installed. Run supabase/patch-12-showcase-ad-labels.sql in the Supabase SQL Editor, then reload this page.'
          : `Could not load showcase content: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }

    const items = (data ?? []) as Showcase[];
    setRows(items);
    setForms(current => ({
      ...current,
      ...Object.fromEntries(items.map(item => [item.slot, {
        ...current[item.slot],
        label: item.label ?? '',
        subLabel: item.sub_label ?? '',
        message: item.message ?? '',
      }])),
    }));
    setMessage('');
  }, [sb]);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  function updateForm(slot: Slot, patch: Partial<Form>) {
    setForms(current => ({ ...current, [slot]: { ...current[slot], ...patch } }));
  }

  async function save(slot: Slot) {
    setMessage('');
    const form = forms[slot];
    const existing = rows.find(row => row.slot === slot);
    if (!form.message.trim()) {
      setMessage(`Add a message for showcase ${slot}.`);
      return;
    }
    if (!form.photo && !existing) {
      setMessage(`Choose an image for showcase ${slot}.`);
      return;
    }

    setBusySlot(slot);
    let imagePath = existing?.image_path ?? null;
    if (form.photo) {
      const upload = await uploadPublic(sb, 'homepage-showcases', form.photo);
      if (upload.error || !upload.path) {
        setBusySlot(null);
        setMessage(`Showcase ${slot} image could not be uploaded: ${upload.error ?? 'No file path was returned.'}`);
        return;
      }
      imagePath = upload.path;
    } else if (form.removePhoto) {
      imagePath = null;
    }

    const { error } = await sb.from('homepage_showcases').upsert({
      slot,
      label: form.label.trim() || null,
      sub_label: form.subLabel.trim() || null,
      message: form.message.trim(),
      image_path: imagePath,
    }, { onConflict: 'slot' });

    if (error) {
      let cleanupWarning = '';
      if (form.photo && imagePath) {
        const { error: cleanupError } = await sb.storage.from('public-media').remove([imagePath]);
        if (cleanupError) {
          console.error(`Unattached showcase ${slot} image could not be removed:`, cleanupError);
          cleanupWarning = ` The uploaded image also could not be removed: ${cleanupError.message}`;
        }
      }
      setBusySlot(null);
      console.error(`Homepage showcase ${slot} could not be saved:`, error);
      setMessage(`Showcase ${slot} could not be saved: ${error.message}${cleanupWarning}`);
      return;
    }

    const oldImage = existing?.image_path;
    let cleanupWarning = '';
    if (form.photo && oldImage) {
      const { error: removeError } = await sb.storage.from('public-media').remove([oldImage]);
      if (removeError) {
        console.error(`Replaced showcase ${slot} image could not be removed:`, removeError);
        cleanupWarning = ` The previous image could not be removed: ${removeError.message}`;
      }
    } else if (form.removePhoto && oldImage) {
      const { error: removeError } = await sb.storage.from('public-media').remove([oldImage]);
      if (removeError) {
        console.error(`Removed showcase ${slot} image could not be deleted:`, removeError);
        cleanupWarning = ` The old image could not be removed: ${removeError.message}`;
      }
    }

    setBusySlot(null);
    playUiSound('success');
    setForms(current => ({
      ...current,
      [slot]: { ...current[slot], photo: null, removePhoto: false, resetKey: current[slot].resetKey + 1 },
    }));
    await load();
    setMessage(`Showcase ${slot} saved.${cleanupWarning}`);
  }

  async function remove(slot: Slot) {
    const existing = rows.find(row => row.slot === slot);
    if (!existing) return;
    if (!confirm(`Delete showcase ${slot}?`)) return;
    setMessage('');
    setBusySlot(slot);
    const { error } = await sb.from('homepage_showcases').delete().eq('slot', slot);
    if (error) {
      setBusySlot(null);
      console.error(`Homepage showcase ${slot} could not be deleted:`, error);
      setMessage(`Showcase ${slot} could not be deleted: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }

    let cleanupMessage = '';
    if (existing.image_path) {
      const { error: removeError } = await sb.storage.from('public-media').remove([existing.image_path]);
      if (removeError) {
        console.error(`Deleted showcase ${slot} image could not be removed:`, removeError);
        cleanupMessage = ` Showcase ${slot} was deleted, but its image could not be removed: ${removeError.message}`;
      }
    }

    setBusySlot(null);
    playUiSound('delete');
    setForms(current => ({ ...current, [slot]: emptyForm() }));
    await load();
    setMessage(cleanupMessage || `Showcase ${slot} deleted.`);
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Add up to three homepage ad/showcase placements. Both labels are optional; a placement appears only when it has an image and message.</p>
      {message && <p role="status" className="text-sm text-gray-700">{message}</p>}
      <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {slots.map(slot => {
          const current = rows.find(row => row.slot === slot);
          const form = forms[slot];
          return (
            <section key={slot} className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-4">
              <h3 className="font-medium">Showcase {slot}</h3>
              <input
                className={input + ' w-full'}
                aria-label={`Showcase ${slot} main label (optional)`}
                placeholder="Main label (optional), e.g. DC-30"
                value={form.label}
                onChange={event => updateForm(slot, { label: event.target.value })}
              />
              <input
                className={input + ' w-full'}
                aria-label={`Showcase ${slot} sub-label (optional)`}
                placeholder="Sub-label (optional), e.g. Ultrasound Machine"
                value={form.subLabel}
                onChange={event => updateForm(slot, { subLabel: event.target.value })}
              />
              <textarea
                className={input + ' w-full'}
                aria-label={`Showcase ${slot} message`}
                placeholder="Message"
                rows={4}
                value={form.message}
                onChange={event => updateForm(slot, { message: event.target.value })}
              />
              <PhotoField
                sb={sb}
                current={current?.image_path ?? null}
                file={form.photo}
                setFile={photo => updateForm(slot, { photo })}
                remove={form.removePhoto}
                setRemove={removePhoto => updateForm(slot, { removePhoto })}
                resetKey={form.resetKey}
              />
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button className={btn} disabled={busySlot !== null} onClick={() => save(slot)}>
                  {busySlot === slot ? 'Saving...' : current ? 'Save changes' : 'Add showcase'}
                </button>
                {current && <button className={dangerBtn} disabled={busySlot !== null} onClick={() => remove(slot)}>Delete</button>}
                {form.photo || form.removePhoto ? <button className={alt} disabled={busySlot !== null} onClick={() => updateForm(slot, { photo: null, removePhoto: false, resetKey: form.resetKey + 1 })}>Cancel image change</button> : null}
              </div>
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/ui';
import { isUpcoming, feeLabel, safeUrl, localToday } from '@/lib/events';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';
import TextReveal from '@/components/TextReveal';

export default function EventView({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const [e, setE] = useState<any>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [today, setToday] = useState('');
  useEffect(() => {
    sb.from('events').select('*').eq('id', id).maybeSingle().then(({ data }) => { setE(data); setToday(localToday()); setDone(true); });
  }, [sb, id]);

  if (!done) return <main className="p-8">Loading...</main>;
  if (!e) return <main className="p-8">Event not found. <Link className="underline" href="/">Home</Link></main>;
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-5 py-8 sm:px-8 sm:py-12">
      <WaveCard className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <article>
      {e.image_path && <img src={sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(e.image_path).data.publicUrl} alt="" className="max-h-[34rem] w-full object-cover" />}
      <div className="p-5 sm:p-9">
      <Link href="/#events" className="text-sm font-medium text-teal-800 hover:text-teal-900">Back to events</Link>
      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-500">{today && <StatusBadge upcoming={isUpcoming(e, today)} />}<span>{e.venue_date}</span></div>
      <h1 className="mt-2 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight sm:text-4xl"><TextReveal>{e.title}</TextReveal></h1>
      <div className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-line text-lg leading-relaxed text-gray-800">{e.description}</div>
      {isUpcoming(e, today) && (
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
          <span className="rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-1">{feeLabel(e)}</span>
          {safeUrl(e.register_url) && <a href={safeUrl(e.register_url)!} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="rounded bg-teal-800 px-5 py-2.5 font-medium text-white">Register Now</a>}
        </div>
      )}
      </div>
      </article>
      </WaveCard>
    </main>
  );
}

===== FILE: components\GoogleOneTap.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { createBrowserClient } from '@supabase/ssr';

declare global {
  interface Window { google?: any; __sruOneTap?: boolean }
}

const GSI_SRC = 'https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client';

// One-time value that ties the Google sign-in to this browser (Supabase checks it).
async function sha256Hex(text: string) {
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(text));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

// Loads Google's script once, even if this component mounts several times.
function loadGoogleScript(): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (window.google?.accounts?.id) return resolve();
    let el = document.querySelector<HTMLScriptElement>(`script[src="${GSI_SRC}"]`);
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('script');
      el.src = GSI_SRC;
      el.async = true;
      document.head.appendChild(el);
    }
    el.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(), { once: true });
    el.addEventListener('error', () => reject(new Error('Could not load Google sign-in')), { once: true });
  });
}

// One-click sign-in for returning visitors. After sign-in the app looks the email up:
// a row in `members` means registered, a row in `staff_roles` means staff, neither means
// the topbar shows "Become a Member".
export default function GoogleOneTap() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const clientId = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID;
    if (!clientId || window.__sruOneTap) return;
    window.__sruOneTap = true;

    const supabase = createBrowserClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!);

    (async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (data.session) return;

      await loadGoogleScript();

      const rawNonce = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32))));
      const hashedNonce = await sha256Hex(rawNonce);

      window.google.accounts.id.initialize({
        client_id: clientId,
        nonce: hashedNonce,
        use_fedcm_for_prompt: true,
        auto_select: true,
        cancel_on_tap_outside: false,
        callback: async ({ credential }: { credential: string }) => {
          const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithIdToken({ provider: 'google', token: credential, nonce: rawNonce });
          if (error) { console.error('Sign-in failed:', error.message); return; }
          location.reload();
        },
      });
      window.google.accounts.id.prompt();
    })().catch(err => {
      console.error(err);
      window.__sruOneTap = false;
    });
  }, []);

  return null;
}

  | 'close'
  | 'bell';

export default function Icon({ name, className = 'h-5 w-5' }: { name: IconName; className?: string }) {

      {paths[name]}
    </svg>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\member\ArticlesSection.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, editBtn, dangerBtn } from './shared';
import PhotoField from '@/components/content/PhotoField';
import { uploadPublic, removePublic } from '@/components/content/shared';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';

type Art = { id: number; title: string; body: string; image_path: string | null; status: string; decline_reason: string | null };
const LABEL: Record<string, [string, string]> = { approved: ['Published', 'text-green-700'], pending: ['Awaiting approval', 'text-amber-700'], declined: ['Declined', 'text-red-700'] };

// Members write articles here. An article is shown publicly only after an admin approves it.
export default function ArticlesSection({ sb, memberId }: { sb: SB; memberId: string }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Art[]>([]);
  const [f, setF] = useState({ title: '', body: '' });
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [remove, setRemove] = useState(false);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await sb.from('articles').select('id,title,body,image_path,status,decline_reason').eq('author_id', memberId).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    setRows(data ?? []);
  }, [sb, memberId]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const editing = editId ? rows.find(r => r.id === editId) : undefined;
  const reset = () => { setEditId(null); setF({ title: '', body: '' }); setPhoto(null); setRemove(false); setRk(k => k + 1); };

  function edit(a: Art) {
    setEditId(a.id); setF({ title: a.title, body: a.body }); setPhoto(null); setRemove(false); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('');
  }

  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!f.title.trim() || !f.body.trim()) return setMsg('Enter a title and the article text.');
    if (editing?.status === 'approved' && !confirm('Saving sends this article for approval again, and it is hidden from visitors until it is approved. Continue?')) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const old = editing?.image_path ?? null;
    let image_path: string | null | undefined;
    if (photo) {
      const r = await uploadPublic(sb as any, memberId, photo);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The photo could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      image_path = r.path!;
    } else if (remove) image_path = null;

    const payload: any = { title: f.title.trim(), body: f.body.trim(), status: 'pending', decline_reason: null };
    if (image_path !== undefined) payload.image_path = image_path;
    if (!editId) payload.author_id = memberId;

    const { error } = editId ? await sb.from('articles').update(payload).eq('id', editId) : await sb.from('articles').insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save the article: ' + error.message);
    if (editId && image_path !== undefined) await removePublic(sb as any, old);
    setMsg('Saved. It will appear on the site once an admin approves it.'); reset(); load();
  }

  async function del(a: Art) {
    if (!confirm('Delete this article?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('articles').delete().eq('id', a.id);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not delete: ' + error.message);
    await removePublic(sb as any, a.image_path); if (editId === a.id) reset(); load();
  }

  return (
    <section>
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">My articles</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 text-gray-600">Articles appear on the site only after an admin approves them. Your name and photo are shown as the author.</p>

      <WaveCard className="mt-4 space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h3 className="font-medium">{editId ? 'Edit article' : 'Write an article'}</h3>
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} placeholder="Title" value={f.title} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, title: e.target.value })} />
        <textarea className={input + ' w-full'} rows={8} placeholder="Your article" value={f.body} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, body: e.target.value })} />
        <PhotoField sb={sb as any} current={editing?.image_path ?? null} file={photo} setFile={setPhoto} remove={remove} setRemove={setRemove} resetKey={rk} />
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : editId ? 'Save changes' : 'Submit for approval'}</button>
          {editId && <button className={alt} onClick={reset}>Cancel</button>}
        </div>
        {msg && <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">{msg}</p>}
      </WaveCard>

      <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-gray-200">
        {rows.map((a, index) => {
          const [label, cls] = LABEL[a.status] ?? [a.status, ''];
          return (
            <li key={a.id}>
              <WaveCard index={index} className="py-3">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-48 flex-1"><b>{a.title}</b> <span className={'text-sm ' + cls}>{label}</span></div>
                {a.status === 'approved' && <Link className={alt} href={`/article?id=${a.id}`}>View</Link>}
                <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => edit(a)}>Edit</button>
                <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => del(a)}>Delete</button>
              </div>
              {a.decline_reason && <div className="text-sm text-gray-600">Reason: {a.decline_reason}</div>}
              </WaveCard>
            </li>
          );
        })}
        {rows.length === 0 && <li className="py-3 text-sm text-gray-500">You have not written any articles yet.</li>}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\member\CareerSection.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, editBtn, dangerBtn, tag, uploadProof, removeProof, viewProof } from './shared';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';

type Car = { id: string; kind: string; organisation: string; title: string; from_year: number; to_year: number | null; status: string; rejection_reason: string | null; proof_path: string | null };
const blank = { kind: 'education', organisation: '', title: '', from_year: '', to_year: '' };

export default function CareerSection({ sb, memberId }: { sb: SB; memberId: string }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Car[]>([]);
  const [f, setF] = useState(blank);
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await sb.from('career_entries').select('id,kind,organisation,title,from_year,to_year,status,rejection_reason,proof_path').eq('member_id', memberId).order('from_year', { ascending: false });
    setRows(data ?? []);
  }, [sb, memberId]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const editing = editId ? rows.find(r => r.id === editId) : undefined;
  const reset = () => { setEditId(null); setF(blank); setFile(null); setRk(k => k + 1); };

  function edit(c: Car) {
    setEditId(c.id); setFile(null); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('');
    setF({ kind: c.kind, organisation: c.organisation, title: c.title, from_year: String(c.from_year), to_year: c.to_year ? String(c.to_year) : '' });
  }

  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    const from = Number(f.from_year), to = f.to_year ? Number(f.to_year) : null;
    if (!f.organisation.trim() || !f.title.trim() || !from) return setMsg('Enter the institution, the title and the start year.');
    if (to && to < from) return setMsg('The end year cannot be before the start year.');
    if (!editId && !file) return setMsg('Attach proof, such as an admission letter or a posting letter.');
    setBusy(true);
    let proof_path: string | undefined;
    if (file) {
      const r = await uploadProof(sb, memberId, file);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The document could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      proof_path = r.path;
    }
    const payload: any = { kind: f.kind, organisation: f.organisation.trim(), title: f.title.trim(), from_year: from, to_year: to };
    if (proof_path) payload.proof_path = proof_path;
    if (editId) Object.assign(payload, { status: 'pending', rejection_reason: null, reviewed_by: null });
    else payload.member_id = memberId;

    const { error } = editId ? await sb.from('career_entries').update(payload).eq('id', editId) : await sb.from('career_entries').insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save the entry: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    if (editId && proof_path) await removeProof(sb, editing?.proof_path);
    setMsg(editId ? 'Updated. It will be checked again.' : 'Added. It shows as verified once the Society checks your proof.'); reset(); load();
  }

  async function remove(c: Car) {
    if (!confirm('Delete this entry?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('career_entries').delete().eq('id', c.id);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not delete: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('delete');
    await removeProof(sb, c.proof_path); if (editId === c.id) reset(); load();
  }

  return (
    <section>
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Career journey</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 text-gray-600">Record your education and each workplace with proof. Verified entries carry the Societyâ€™s mark on your CV.</p>

      <WaveCard className="mt-4 space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h3 className="font-medium">{editId ? 'Edit entry' : 'Add an entry'}</h3>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <select className={input} value={f.kind} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, kind: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="education">Education</option><option value="employment">Employment</option>
          </select>
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="School or hospital" value={f.organisation} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, organisation: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Programme or job title" value={f.title} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, title: e.target.value })} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input className={input + ' w-32'} type="number" placeholder="From year" value={f.from_year} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, from_year: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input + ' w-44'} type="number" placeholder="To year, blank if current" value={f.to_year} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, to_year: e.target.value })} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-sm">
          <label>{editId ? 'Replace proof (optional)' : 'Proof (admission or posting letter)'} <input key={rk} type="file" accept="image/*,.pdf" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} /></label>
          <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : editId ? 'Save changes' : 'Add entry'}</button>
          {editId && <button className={alt} onClick={reset}>Cancel</button>}
        </div>
        {msg && <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">{msg}</p>}
      </WaveCard>

      <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-gray-200">
        {rows.map((c, index) => (
          <li key={c.id}>
            <WaveCard index={index} className="py-3">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <div className="min-w-48 flex-1"><b>{c.title}</b>, {c.organisation} ({c.from_year} to {c.to_year ?? 'present'}) <span className={tag(c.status)}>{c.status}</span></div>
              {c.proof_path && <button className={alt} onClick={() => viewProof(sb, c.proof_path)}>View proof</button>}
              {c.status !== 'verified' && <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => edit(c)}>Edit</button>}
              {c.status !== 'verified' && <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => remove(c)}>Delete</button>}
            </div>
            {c.rejection_reason && <div className="text-sm text-gray-600">Reason: {c.rejection_reason}</div>}
            {c.status === 'verified' && <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">Verified entries are locked. Contact the Society to correct one.</div>}
            </WaveCard>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\member\CpdSection.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn, alt, editBtn, dangerBtn, tag, uploadProof, removeProof, viewProof } from './shared';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';

type Cpd = { id: string; title: string; points: number; year: number; status: string; rejection_reason: string | null; proof_path: string | null; added_by_staff: boolean };

export default function CpdSection({ sb, memberId, min }: { sb: SB; memberId: string; min: number }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Cpd[]>([]);
  const [year, setYear] = useState(0);
  const [f, setF] = useState({ title: '', points: '' });
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await sb.from('cpd_entries').select('id,title,points,year,status,rejection_reason,proof_path,added_by_staff').eq('member_id', memberId).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    setRows(data ?? []); setYear(new Date().getFullYear());
  }, [sb, memberId]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const editing = editId ? rows.find(r => r.id === editId) : undefined;
  const reset = () => { setEditId(null); setF({ title: '', points: '' }); setFile(null); setRk(k => k + 1); };
  const locked = (r: Cpd) => r.status === 'verified' || r.added_by_staff;

  function edit(r: Cpd) {
    setEditId(r.id); setF({ title: r.title, points: String(r.points) }); setFile(null); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('');
  }

  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    const points = Number(f.points);
    if (!f.title.trim() || !points || points < 1) return setMsg('Enter the activity title and the points claimed.');
    if (!editId && !file) return setMsg('Attach your certificate as proof.');
    setBusy(true);
    let proof_path: string | undefined;
    if (file) {
      const r = await uploadProof(sb, memberId, file);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The certificate could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      proof_path = r.path;
    }
    const payload: any = { title: f.title.trim(), points };
    if (proof_path) payload.proof_path = proof_path;
    if (editId) Object.assign(payload, { status: 'pending', rejection_reason: null, reviewed_by: null });   // an edited entry is checked again
    else payload.member_id = memberId;

    const { error } = editId ? await sb.from('cpd_entries').update(payload).eq('id', editId) : await sb.from('cpd_entries').insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save the entry: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    if (editId && proof_path) await removeProof(sb, editing?.proof_path);
    setMsg(editId ? 'Updated. It will be checked again.' : 'Submitted. It counts once a coordinator verifies it.'); reset(); load();
  }

  async function remove(r: Cpd) {
    if (!confirm('Delete this CPD entry?')) return;
    const { error } = await sb.from('cpd_entries').delete().eq('id', r.id);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not delete: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('delete');
    await removeProof(sb, r.proof_path); if (editId === r.id) reset(); load();
  }

  const pts = rows.filter(c => c.status === 'verified' && c.year === year).reduce((a, c) => a + c.points, 0);
  const total = rows.filter(c => c.status === 'verified').reduce((a, c) => a + c.points, 0);

  return (
    <section>
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">CPD points</h2>
      <p className="mt-1">{pts}{min ? ` of ${min}` : ''} verified points in {year || ''}. All years: {total}. Points restart each year.</p>
      {min > 0 && <div className="mt-2 h-2 border border-gray-300"><div className="h-full bg-teal-800" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (pts / min) * 100)}%` }} /></div>}

      <WaveCard className="mt-4 space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <h3 className="font-medium">{editId ? 'Edit CPD activity' : 'Submit a CPD activity'}</h3>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Activity title" value={f.title} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, title: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input + ' w-32'} type="number" min="1" placeholder="Points" value={f.points} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, points: e.target.value })} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-sm">
          <label>{editId ? 'Replace certificate (optional)' : 'Certificate'} <input key={rk} type="file" accept="image/*,.pdf" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} /></label>
          <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : editId ? 'Save changes' : 'Submit for verification'}</button>
          {editId && <button className={alt} onClick={reset}>Cancel</button>}
        </div>
        {msg && <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">{msg}</p>}
      </WaveCard>

      <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-gray-200">
        {rows.map((c, index) => (
          <li key={c.id}>
            <WaveCard index={index} className="py-3">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <div className="min-w-48 flex-1">{c.title} ({c.year}), {c.points} points. <span className={tag(c.status)}>{c.status}</span></div>
              {c.proof_path && <button className={alt} onClick={() => viewProof(sb, c.proof_path)}>View certificate</button>}
              {!locked(c) && <button className={editBtn} onClick={() => edit(c)}>Edit</button>}
              {!locked(c) && <button className={dangerBtn} onClick={() => remove(c)}>Delete</button>}
            </div>
            {c.rejection_reason && <div className="text-sm text-gray-600">Reason: {c.rejection_reason}</div>}
            {locked(c) && <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">Verified entries are locked. Contact the Society to correct one.</div>}
            </WaveCard>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\member\ProfileSection.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { SB, input, btn } from './shared';
import PhotoField from '@/components/content/PhotoField';
import { uploadPublic, removePublic } from '@/components/content/shared';
import { playUiSound } from '@/lib/ui-sound';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';

// The member's own details. They appear on the CV and on articles the member writes.
export default function ProfileSection({ sb, memberId }: { sb: SB; memberId: string }) {
  const [f, setF] = useState({ full_name: '', phone: '', workplace: '', bio: '' });
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [remove, setRemove] = useState(false);
  const [rk, setRk] = useState(0);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await sb.from('members').select('full_name,phone,workplace,bio,avatar_path').eq('id', memberId).maybeSingle();
    if (!data) return;
    setF({ full_name: data.full_name ?? '', phone: data.phone ?? '', workplace: data.workplace ?? '', bio: data.bio ?? '' });
    setAvatar(data.avatar_path ?? null);
  }, [sb, memberId]);
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  async function save() {
    setMsg('');
    if (!f.full_name.trim()) return setMsg('Your name cannot be empty.');
    setBusy(true);
    const old = avatar;
    let avatar_path: string | null | undefined;
    if (file) {
      const r = await uploadPublic(sb, memberId, file);
      if (r.error) { setBusy(false); return setMsg('The photo could not be uploaded: ' + r.error); }
      avatar_path = r.path!;
    } else if (remove) avatar_path = null;

    const payload: any = { full_name: f.full_name.trim(), phone: f.phone.trim() || null, workplace: f.workplace.trim() || null, bio: f.bio.trim() || null };
    if (avatar_path !== undefined) payload.avatar_path = avatar_path;
    const { error } = await sb.from('members').update(payload).eq('id', memberId);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return setMsg('Could not save: ' + error.message);
    playUiSound('success');
    if (avatar_path !== undefined) await removePublic(sb, old);
    setFile(null); setRemove(false); setRk(k => k + 1); setMsg('Saved.'); load();
  }

  return (
    <section>
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">My details</h2>
      <WaveCard className="mt-3 space-y-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <input className={input + ' w-full'} placeholder="Full name" value={f.full_name} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, full_name: e.target.value })} />
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Phone" value={f.phone} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, phone: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input + ' flex-1'} placeholder="Workplace or school" value={f.workplace} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, workplace: e.target.value })} />
        </div>
        <textarea className={input + ' w-full'} rows={3} placeholder="A short bio" value={f.bio} onChange={e => setF({ ...f, bio: e.target.value })} />
        <PhotoField sb={sb as any} current={avatar} file={file} setFile={setFile} remove={remove} setRemove={setRemove} resetKey={rk} round />
        <button className={btn} disabled={busy} onClick={save}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : 'Save details'}</button>
        {msg && <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">{msg}</p>}
      </WaveCard>
    </section>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\member\shared.ts =====

import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';

export type SB = ReturnType<typeof browser>;
export const input = 'rounded border border-gray-300 p-2';
export const btn = 'button-primary px-4 py-2 disabled:opacity-50';
export const alt = 'button-neutral px-3 py-1.5 text-sm disabled:opacity-50';
export const editBtn = 'button-edit px-3 py-1.5 text-sm disabled:opacity-50';
export const successBtn = 'button-success px-3 py-1.5 text-sm disabled:opacity-50';
export const dangerBtn = 'button-danger px-3 py-1.5 text-sm disabled:opacity-50';
export const tag = (s: string) => (s === 'verified' ? 'text-green-700' : s === 'rejected' ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-amber-700');

// Uploads a private proof document into the member's own folder.
export async function uploadProof(sb: SB, memberId: string, file: File) {
  const path = `${memberId}/${Date.now()}-${file.name.replace(/[^\w.\-]/g, '_')}`;
  const { error } = await sb.storage.from('proofs').upload(path, file);
  return error ? { error: error.message } : { path };
}
export async function removeProof(sb: SB, path: string | null | undefined) {
  if (path) await sb.storage.from('proofs').remove([path]);
}
export async function viewProof(sb: SB, path: string | null) {
  if (!path) return;
  const { data } = await sb.storage.from('proofs').createSignedUrl(path, 300);
  if (data?.signedUrl) window.open(data.signedUrl, '_blank');
}



===== FILE: components\ProfileView.tsx =====

'use client';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import { Avatar, fmt } from '@/components/ui';
import WaveCard from '@/components/WaveCard';
import TextReveal from '@/components/TextReveal';

type PublicProfile = {
  id: string;
  full_name: string;
  workplace: string | null;
  bio: string | null;
  avatar_path: string | null;
};

type PublishedArticle = {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  created_at: string;
};

// A member's public profile: what any visitor may see. Private details never appear here.
export default function ProfileView({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const pub = (p: string) => sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(p).data.publicUrl;
  const [p, setP] = useState<PublicProfile | null>(null);
  const [arts, setArts] = useState<PublishedArticle[]>([]);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    (async () => {
      const [a, b] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('public_profiles').select('id,full_name,workplace,bio,avatar_path').eq('id', id).maybeSingle(),
        sb.from('articles').select('id,title,body,created_at').eq('author_id', id).eq('status', 'approved').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      ]);
      if (!active) return;
      if (a.error || b.error) {
        const cause = a.error ?? b.error;
        console.error('Public profile could not be loaded:', cause);
        setError(`Could not load this profile: ${cause.message}`);
      } else {
        setP(a.data as PublicProfile | null);
        setArts((b.data ?? []) as PublishedArticle[]);
      }
      setDone(true);
    })();
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [sb, id]);

  if (!done) return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6">
      <div className="animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
        <div className="h-1 w-full rounded-full bg-gray-100" />
        <div className="mt-5 flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="h-16 w-16 rounded-full bg-gray-100" />
          <div className="space-y-2"><div className="h-5 w-48 rounded bg-gray-100" /><div className="h-3 w-32 rounded bg-gray-100" /></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
  if (error) return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6">
      <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-red-800 shadow-sm">{error}</div>
      <Link className="mt-4 inline-flex text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-black" href="/">â† Back to home</Link>
    </main>
  );
  if (!p) return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6">
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-6 text-center shadow-sm sm:p-10">
        <p className="gradient-accent-text text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">SRU community</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold">Profile not found</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">This profile may no longer be available.</p>
        <Link className="mt-4 inline-flex rounded-lg bg-gray-900 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-black" href="/">Back to home</Link>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-5xl px-4 py-5 sm:px-6 sm:py-8">
      <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 transition hover:text-gray-950">â† Home</Link>

      <WaveCard className="gradient-card mt-3 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <div className="h-20 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-950 via-gray-800 to-gray-950 sm:h-28" />
        <div className="px-4 pb-4 sm:px-6 sm:pb-6">
          <div className="-mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 sm:-mt-10 sm:gap-4">
            <div className="rounded-full bg-white p-1 shadow-md">
              <Avatar name={p.full_name} src={p.avatar_path ? pub(p.avatar_path) : ''} size="h-16 w-16 sm:h-20 sm:w-20" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 pb-1">
              <p className="gradient-accent-text text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">SRU member profile</p>
              <h1 className="mt-0.5 break-words text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight sm:text-2xl"><TextReveal>{p.full_name}</TextReveal></h1>
              {p.workplace && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">{p.workplace}</p>}
            </div>
          </div>
          {p.bio && (
            <div className="mt-4 border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
              <h2 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">About</h2>
              <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-6 text-gray-700">{p.bio}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </WaveCard>

      <WaveCard className="mt-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <section>
        <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-gray-100 px-4 py-3 sm:px-5">
          <div>
            <p className="gradient-accent-text text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">Published writing</p>
            <h2 className="mt-0.5 text-base font-semibold">Articles</h2>
          </div>
          <span className="rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-gray-600">{arts.length}</span>
        </header>
        <ul className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
          {arts.map((a, i) => (
            <li key={a.id}>
              <WaveCard index={i} className="px-4 py-3 transition-colors hover:bg-gray-50/80 sm:px-5">
              <Link href={`/article?id=${a.id}`} className="font-medium text-gray-900 underline-offset-4 hover:text-indigo-700 hover:underline">{a.title}</Link>
              <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-5 text-gray-600">{a.body}</p>
              <div className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] text-gray-500">{fmt(a.created_at)}</div>
              </WaveCard>
            </li>
          ))}
          {arts.length === 0 && (
            <li className="px-4 py-8 text-center sm:px-5">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-800">No published articles yet</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Published contributions from this member will appear here.</p>
            </li>
          )}
        </ul>
      </section>
      </WaveCard>
    </main>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\PublicMotionScope.tsx =====

'use client';

import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export default function PublicMotionScope() {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  useEffect(() => {
    const isAdminRoute = pathname === '/admin' || pathname.startsWith('/admin/');
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle('motion-enabled', !isAdminRoute);
  }, [pathname]);

  return null;
}
