'use client';

import type { HTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useInView } from '@/lib/use-in-view';

type Props = HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> & { children: ReactNode };

export default function RevealSection({ children, className = '', ...props }: Props) {
  const { ref, isVisible } = useInView<HTMLElement>();

  return (
    <section
      {...props}
      ref={ref}
      data-motion-visible={isVisible}
      className={`reveal-section ${isVisible ? 'is-visible' : ''} ${className}`}
    >
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\SiteSearch.tsx =====

'use client';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';

type SearchResult = { href: string; title: string; detail: string; kind: string };

export default function SiteSearch() {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [results, setResults] = useState<SearchResult[]>([]);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const searchRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    inputRef.current?.focus();
    const closeOnOutsideClick = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (event.target instanceof Node && !searchRef.current?.contains(event.target)) setOpen(false);
    };
    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key !== 'Escape') return;
      setOpen(false);
      triggerRef.current?.focus();
    };
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', closeOnOutsideClick);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', closeOnEscape);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', closeOnOutsideClick);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', closeOnEscape);
    };
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const term = query.trim().replace(/[%_]/g, '');
    if (term.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      setMessage('');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);
    const timer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      const pattern = `%${term}%`;
      const [articles, events, profiles] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('articles').select('id,title').eq('status', 'approved').ilike('title', pattern).limit(4),
        sb.from('events').select('id,title,venue_date').ilike('title', pattern).limit(3),
        sb.from('public_profiles').select('id,full_name,workplace').ilike('full_name', pattern).limit(3),
      ]);
      if (!active) return;

      const errors = [articles.error, events.error, profiles.error].filter(Boolean);
      if (errors.length) {
        console.error('Site search failed:', errors);
        setMessage('Some search results could not be loaded. Please try again.');
      } else {
        setMessage('');
      }

      const items: SearchResult[] = [
        ...(articles.data ?? []).map(row => ({ href: `/article?id=${row.id}`, title: row.title, detail: 'Article', kind: 'Article' })),
        ...(events.data ?? []).map(row => ({ href: `/event?id=${row.id}`, title: row.title, detail: row.venue_date ?? 'Event', kind: 'Event' })),
        ...(profiles.data ?? []).map(row => ({ href: `/profile?id=${row.id}`, title: row.full_name, detail: row.workplace ?? 'Member profile', kind: 'Member' })),
      ];
      setResults(items);
      if (!items.length && !errors.length) setMessage('No matching articles, events, or member profiles.');
      setLoading(false);
    }, 250);
    return () => {
      active = false;
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [query, sb]);

  return (
    <div ref={searchRef} className="relative">
      <button
        ref={triggerRef}
        type="button"
        aria-label="Search the SRU website"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls="site-search-panel"
        data-tooltip="Search"
        onClick={() => setOpen(value => !value)}
        className="tooltip-trigger inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#3a3b43] bg-[#1b1c22] text-gray-300 transition hover:border-gray-500 hover:bg-[#292a31] hover:text-white"
      >
        <Icon name="search" className="h-4 w-4" />
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div id="site-search-panel" className="absolute right-0 top-full z-[70] mt-2 w-[min(22rem,calc(100vw-2rem))] max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-2 shadow-xl">
          <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="site-search">Search the SRU website</label>
          <div data-site-search-box className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-3 transition focus-within:border-gray-900 focus-within:bg-white focus-within:ring-0">
            <Icon name="search" className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-gray-500" />
            <input
              ref={inputRef}
              id="site-search"
              type="search"
              value={query}
              onChange={event => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search articles, events, members"
              autoComplete="off"
              className="min-w-0 flex-1 border-0 bg-transparent py-2 text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none ring-0 placeholder:text-gray-500 focus:border-0 focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
              aria-expanded={query.trim().length >= 2}
              aria-controls="site-search-results"
              role="combobox"
              aria-autocomplete="list"
            />
            <kbd className="hidden rounded border border-gray-200 bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-gray-500 sm:inline">Esc</kbd>
          </div>
          {query.trim().length >= 2 && (
            <div id="site-search-results" role="listbox" className="mt-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto">
              {loading && <p className="px-3 py-3 text-sm text-gray-500">Searchingâ€¦</p>}
              {!loading && results.map(result => (
                <Link
                  key={`${result.kind}:${result.href}`}
                  href={result.href}
                  role="option"
                  onClick={() => { setOpen(false); setQuery(''); }}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-gray-50"
                >
                  <span className="min-w-0">
                    <span className="block truncate text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">{result.title}</span>
                    <span className="block truncate text-xs text-gray-500">{result.detail}</span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-600">{result.kind}</span>
                </Link>
              ))}
              {!loading && message && <p className={`px-3 py-3 text-sm ${message.startsWith('No matching') ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-red-700'}`}>{message}</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\TextReveal.tsx =====

'use client';

import type { CSSProperties, HTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useInView } from '@/lib/use-in-view';

type Props = HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> & { children: ReactNode; delay?: number };

export default function TextReveal({ children, className = '', delay = 0, style, ...props }: Props) {
  const { ref, isVisible } = useInView<HTMLSpanElement>(0.08);

  return (
    <span
      {...props}
      ref={ref}
      data-motion-visible={isVisible}
      style={{ ...style, '--text-reveal-delay': `${delay}ms` } as CSSProperties}
      className={`text-mask-reveal ${isVisible ? 'is-visible' : ''} ${className}`}
    >
      <span>{children}</span>
    </span>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\TopBar.tsx =====

'use client';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { browser } from '@/lib/browser';
import { Avatar } from '@/components/ui';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import SiteSearch from '@/components/SiteSearch';
import { playUiSound, primeUiSounds } from '@/lib/ui-sound';

type S = {
  loaded: boolean;
  signedIn: boolean;
  member: { id: string; full_name: string; title: string | null; avatar_path: string | null; paid_through_year: number } | null;
  staff: string | null;
  logo: string;
};
type Notice = { key: string; kind: 'article' | 'event' | 'renewal'; title: string; href: string; isNew: boolean };
type NoticeState = { items: Notice[]; unread: number; newestArticleId: number; newestEventId: number; renewalDue: boolean; renewalYear: number };

const mainLinks = [
  { label: 'Home', href: '/', icon: 'home' as const },
  { label: 'News', href: '/#news', icon: 'article' as const },
  { label: 'Events', href: '/#events', icon: 'calendar' as const },
  { label: 'Committee', href: '/committee', icon: 'users' as const },
];

export default function TopBar() {
  const sb = useMemo(() => browser(), []);
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [s, setS] = useState<S>({ loaded: false, signedIn: false, member: null, staff: null, logo: '' });
  const [noticeState, setNoticeState] = useState<NoticeState>({ items: [], unread: 0, newestArticleId: 0, newestEventId: 0, renewalDue: false, renewalYear: 0 });
  const [soundsEnabled, setSoundsEnabled] = useState(true);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [accountOpen, setAccountOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notificationsOpen, setNotificationsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [greeting, setGreeting] = useState('');
  const [currentHash, setCurrentHash] = useState('');
  const closeAccountRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accountOpen) return;
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setAccountOpen(false);
      if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;
      const dialog = closeAccountRef.current?.closest('[role="dialog"]');
      const focusable = dialog?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('a[href], button:not([disabled])');
      if (!focusable?.length) return;
      const first = focusable[0];
      const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    document.addEventListener('keydown', closeOnEscape);
    closeAccountRef.current?.focus();
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', closeOnEscape);
      returnFocusRef.current?.focus();
    };
  }, [accountOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!notificationsOpen) return;
    const closeOnOutsideClick = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (event.target instanceof Element && !event.target.closest('[data-notification-control]')) setNotificationsOpen(false);
    };
    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setNotificationsOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', closeOnOutsideClick);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', closeOnEscape);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', closeOnOutsideClick);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', closeOnEscape);
    };
  }, [notificationsOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    (async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await sb.auth.getUser();
      const { data: lg } = await sb.from('site_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'logo_path').maybeSingle();
      let member: S['member'] = null;
      let staff: string | null = null;
      if (user) {
        const [m, r] = await Promise.all([
          sb.from('members').select('id,full_name,title,avatar_path,paid_through_year').eq('id', user.id).maybeSingle(),
          sb.rpc('my_role'),
        ]);
        let memberData = m.data;
        if (m.error) {
          console.error('Member header details could not be loaded with professional fields:', m.error);
          const fallback = await sb.from('members').select('id,full_name,avatar_path,paid_through_year').eq('id', user.id).maybeSingle();
          if (fallback.error) console.error('Member header details could not be loaded:', fallback.error);
          else memberData = fallback.data ? { ...fallback.data, title: null } : null;
        }
        if (memberData?.paid_through_year != null) {
          member = { id: memberData.id, full_name: memberData.full_name, title: memberData.title, avatar_path: memberData.avatar_path, paid_through_year: memberData.paid_through_year };
        }
        staff = (r.data as string) ?? null;
      }
      const [articles, events] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('articles').select('id,title').eq('status', 'approved').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(20),
        sb.from('events').select('id,title').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(20),
      ]);
      if (articles.error) console.error('Article notifications could not be loaded:', articles.error);
      if (events.error) console.error('Event notifications could not be loaded:', events.error);
      const articleRows = articles.data ?? [];
      const eventRows = events.data ?? [];
      const newestArticleId = articleRows.reduce((max, row) => Math.max(max, Number(row.id)), 0);
      const newestEventId = eventRows.reduce((max, row) => Math.max(max, Number(row.id)), 0);
      const storedArticleId = window.localStorage.getItem('sru-last-read-article-id');
      const storedEventId = window.localStorage.getItem('sru-last-read-event-id');
      const lastArticleId = storedArticleId === null ? newestArticleId : Number(storedArticleId);
      const lastEventId = storedEventId === null ? newestEventId : Number(storedEventId);
      if (storedArticleId === null) window.localStorage.setItem('sru-last-read-article-id', String(newestArticleId));
      if (storedEventId === null) window.localStorage.setItem('sru-last-read-event-id', String(newestEventId));
      const today = new Date();
      const renewalYear = today.getFullYear();
      const renewalDue = !!member && (member.paid_through_year < renewalYear || (member.paid_through_year === renewalYear && today.getMonth() >= 10));
      const renewalRead = window.localStorage.getItem('sru-renewal-read-year') === String(renewalYear);
      const items: Notice[] = [
        ...articleRows.slice(0, 3).map(row => ({ key: `article-${row.id}`, kind: 'article' as const, title: row.title, href: `/article?id=${row.id}`, isNew: Number(row.id) > lastArticleId })),
        ...eventRows.slice(0, 3).map(row => ({ key: `event-${row.id}`, kind: 'event' as const, title: row.title, href: `/event?id=${row.id}`, isNew: Number(row.id) > lastEventId })),
      ];
      if (renewalDue) items.unshift({ key: 'renewal', kind: 'renewal', title: 'Your membership renewal is due.', href: '/member', isNew: !renewalRead });
      const unread = articleRows.filter(row => Number(row.id) > lastArticleId).length
        + eventRows.filter(row => Number(row.id) > lastEventId).length
        + (renewalDue && !renewalRead ? 1 : 0);
      if (!active) return;
      setS({
        loaded: true,
        signedIn: !!user,
        member,
        staff,
        logo: lg?.value ? sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(lg.value).data.publicUrl : '',
      });
      setNoticeState({
        items,
        unread,
        newestArticleId,
        newestEventId,
        renewalDue,
        renewalYear,
      });
    })();
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [sb]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSoundsEnabled(window.localStorage.getItem('sru-ui-sounds') !== 'off');
    const hour = new Date().getHours();
    setGreeting(hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' : hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' : 'Good evening');
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', primeUiSounds, { once: true });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', primeUiSounds, { once: true });
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', primeUiSounds);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', primeUiSounds);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const syncHash = () => setCurrentHash(window.location.hash);
    syncHash();
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncHash);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', syncHash);
  }, []);

  async function signOut() {
    await sb.auth.signOut();
    location.href = '/';
  }

  function toggleNotifications() {
    const opening = !notificationsOpen;
    setNotificationsOpen(opening);
    if (!opening) return;
    if (noticeState.unread > 0) playUiSound('attention');
    window.localStorage.setItem('sru-last-read-article-id', String(noticeState.newestArticleId));
    window.localStorage.setItem('sru-last-read-event-id', String(noticeState.newestEventId));
    if (noticeState.renewalDue) window.localStorage.setItem('sru-renewal-read-year', String(noticeState.renewalYear));
    setNoticeState(state => ({
      ...state,
      unread: 0,
      items: state.items.map(item => ({ ...item, isNew: false })),
    }));
  }

  const greetingName = s.member
    ? `${s.member.title ? `${s.member.title.replace(/\.$/, '')}. ` : ''}${s.member.full_name.trim().replace(/^(?:Dr\.?|Mr\.?|Ms\.?)\s+/i, '').split(/\s+/)[0]}`
    : '';
  const link = 'top-nav-link inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-transparent px-2.5 py-2 text-sm text-gray-300 transition hover:bg-[#23242a] hover:text-white';
  const isActiveLink = (href: string) => {
    const [targetPath, targetHash] = href.split('#');
    const pathMatches = pathname === (targetPath || '/');
    return pathMatches && (targetHash ? currentHash === `#${targetHash}` : !currentHash);
  };
  const navClass = (href: string) => `${link} ${isActiveLink(href) ? '!border-white !bg-white !text-gray-950 [&_svg]:!text-gray-950' : ''}`;
  const notificationControl = (notificationId: string) => (
    <div data-notification-control className="relative">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Notifications"
          aria-expanded={notificationsOpen}
          aria-controls={notificationId}
          title={noticeState.unread ? `${noticeState.unread} new notifications` : 'Notifications'}
          onClick={toggleNotifications}
          className="relative inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#3a3b43] bg-[#1b1c22] text-gray-300 transition hover:border-gray-500 hover:bg-[#292a31] hover:text-white"
        >
          <Icon name="bell" className="h-4 w-4" />
          {noticeState.unread > 0 && <span className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 inline-flex min-w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-600 px-1 text-[10px] font-semibold leading-4 text-white">{noticeState.unread > 9 ? '9+' : noticeState.unread}</span>}
        </button>
        {notificationsOpen && (
          <section id={notificationId} role="dialog" aria-label="Notifications" className="absolute right-0 top-11 z-[70] w-[min(21rem,calc(100vw-1.5rem))] overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-xl">
            <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-gray-100 px-3 py-2.5">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">Notifications</h2>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setNotificationsOpen(false)} aria-label="Close notifications" className="rounded p-1 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-900"><Icon name="close" className="h-4 w-4" /></button>
            </header>
            <ul className="max-h-80 divide-y divide-gray-100 overflow-y-auto">
              {noticeState.items.map(item => (
                <li key={item.key}>
                  <Link href={item.href} onClick={() => setNotificationsOpen(false)} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm hover:bg-gray-50">
                    <Icon name={item.kind === 'article' ? 'article' : item.kind === 'event' ? 'calendar' : 'shield'} className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-gray-500" />
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <span className="block font-medium text-gray-800">
                        {item.kind === 'article' ? item.isNew ? 'New article' : 'Article'
                          : item.kind === 'event' ? item.isNew ? 'New event' : 'Event'
                            : 'Membership renewal'}
                      </span>
                      <span className="mt-0.5 block truncate text-xs text-gray-600">{item.title}</span>
                    </span>
                    {item.isNew && <span className="mt-1 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-blue-600" aria-label="New" />}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
              {noticeState.items.length === 0 && <li className="px-3 py-5 text-center text-xs text-gray-500">Youâ€™re all caught up. New articles and events will appear here.</li>}
            </ul>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-gray-100 px-3 py-2">
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">Soft sound for new alerts</span>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => {
                const next = window.localStorage.getItem('sru-ui-sounds') === 'off' ? 'on' : 'off';
                window.localStorage.setItem('sru-ui-sounds', next);
                setSoundsEnabled(next === 'on');
              }} className="rounded-md border border-gray-200 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50" aria-pressed={soundsEnabled}>
                Sounds {soundsEnabled ? 'on' : 'off'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
    </div>
  );
  const profileControl = s.loaded && s.member && (
        <button type="button" onClick={event => {
          returnFocusRef.current = event.currentTarget;
          setAccountOpen(true);
        }} aria-label="Open profile and membership menu" title="Profile and membership" className="profile-trigger flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full py-0.5 pl-2 pr-0.5 ring-offset-2 transition hover:bg-[#23242a] hover:ring-1 hover:ring-gray-500">
          <span className="hidden text-right leading-tight sm:block">
            <span className="block text-[10px] text-gray-400">{greeting}</span>
            <span className="block max-w-36 truncate text-xs font-semibold text-white">{greetingName}</span>
          </span>
          <Avatar name={s.member.full_name} src={s.member.avatar_path ? sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(s.member.avatar_path).data.publicUrl : ''} size="h-9 w-9" />
        </button>
  );
  const accountLinks = (
    <>
      {s.loaded && s.staff && <Link href="/admin" aria-current={pathname === '/admin' ? 'page' : undefined} className="button-neutral px-3 py-2 text-sm"><Icon name="shield" className="h-4 w-4" />Admin</Link>}
      {(s.staff === 'chief_admin' || s.staff === 'admin') && <Link href="/admin/content" aria-current={pathname === '/admin/content' ? 'page' : undefined} className="button-neutral px-3 py-2 text-sm"><Icon name="article" className="h-4 w-4" />Content</Link>}
      {s.loaded && !s.member && !s.staff && <Link href="/join" className="button-primary rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold shadow-sm">Join SRU</Link>}
      {s.signedIn && <button onClick={signOut} className="rounded-lg px-2 py-2 text-sm text-gray-500 underline-offset-4 hover:text-gray-900 hover:underline">Sign out</button>}
    </>
  );

  return (
    <header className="site-header sticky top-0 z-50 border-b-0 bg-[#101116] text-white shadow-sm backdrop-blur-xl">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[1440px] flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-2 px-3 py-2 sm:px-5 lg:px-7">
        <Link href="/" aria-label="Society of Radiography of Uganda" className="mr-auto flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 font-brand text-sm font-bold tracking-[0.055em] text-white sm:text-base lg:text-lg">
          {s.logo && <img src={s.logo} alt="" className="h-9 w-auto object-contain" />}
          <span className="leading-tight sm:hidden">SRU</span>
          <span className="hidden leading-tight sm:inline">Society of Radiography of Uganda</span>
        </Link>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={`menu-toggle order-2 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition md:hidden ${menuOpen ? 'border-white bg-white text-gray-950' : 'border-[#3a3b43] bg-[#1b1c22] text-gray-200'}`}
          aria-expanded={menuOpen}
          aria-controls="mobile-main-navigation"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(open => !open)}
        >
          <Icon name={menuOpen ? 'close' : 'menu'} className="h-4 w-4" />
          {menuOpen ? 'Close' : 'Menu'}
        </button>
        <div className="order-2 flex items-center gap-2 md:hidden">
          {profileControl}
        </div>
        <nav aria-label="Main navigation" className="hidden items-center gap-0.5 lg:flex">
          {mainLinks.map(item => (
            <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} aria-current={isActiveLink(item.href) ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => setCurrentHash(item.href.includes('#') ? `#${item.href.split('#')[1]}` : '')} className={navClass(item.href)}>
              <Icon name={item.icon} className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" />{item.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
          <Link href="/#contacts" aria-current={isActiveLink('/#contacts') ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => setCurrentHash('#contacts')} className={navClass('/#contacts')}>Contact</Link>
        </nav>
        <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
          {profileControl}
          {accountLinks}
        </div>
        {menuOpen && (
          <div id="mobile-main-navigation" className="order-4 basis-full border-t border-[#3a3b43] pt-3 md:hidden">
            <nav aria-label="Mobile main navigation" className="grid gap-1 sm:grid-cols-2">
              {mainLinks.map(item => (
                <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} aria-current={isActiveLink(item.href) ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => { setCurrentHash(item.href.includes('#') ? `#${item.href.split('#')[1]}` : ''); setMenuOpen(false); }} className={navClass(item.href)}>
                  <Icon name={item.icon} className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" />{item.label}
                </Link>
              ))}
              <Link href="/#contacts" aria-current={isActiveLink('/#contacts') ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => { setCurrentHash('#contacts'); setMenuOpen(false); }} className={navClass('/#contacts')}>Contact</Link>
            </nav>
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-[#303139] pt-3 text-sm">{accountLinks}</div>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="order-2 ml-auto flex items-center gap-2 md:order-last md:ml-0">
          <SiteSearch />
          {notificationControl('site-notifications')}
        </div>
      </div>
      {accountOpen && s.member && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/45 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onMouseDown={event => {
          if (event.target === event.currentTarget) setAccountOpen(false);
        }}>
          <section role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="account-dialog-title" className="w-full max-w-sm animate-rise-in rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-2xl">
            <header className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-gray-100 pb-3">
              <Avatar name={s.member.full_name} src={s.member.avatar_path ? sb.storage.from('public-media').getPublicUrl(s.member.avatar_path).data.publicUrl : ''} size="h-11 w-11" />
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <h2 id="account-dialog-title" className="truncate text-base font-semibold">{s.member.full_name}</h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Member account</p>
              </div>
              <button ref={closeAccountRef} type="button" onClick={() => setAccountOpen(false)} aria-label="Close account menu" className="rounded-lg p-2 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-900">
                <Icon name="close" className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </header>
            <nav aria-label="Member account" className="grid gap-1 pt-3">
              <Link href={`/profile?id=${s.member.id}`} onClick={() => setAccountOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50">
                <Icon name="users" className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500" />Public profile
              </Link>
              <Link href="/member" onClick={() => setAccountOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50">
                <Icon name="shield" className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500" />Membership workspace
              </Link>
            </nav>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\ui.tsx =====

export const fmt = (s: string) => new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });

export function Avatar({ name, src, size = 'h-10 w-10' }: { name: string; src?: string; size?: string }) {
  const ini = name.replace(/[^A-Za-z ]/g, ' ').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(-2).map(w => w[0]).join('').toUpperCase();
  return src
    ? <img src={src} alt="" className={`${size} rounded-full object-cover`} />
    : <span className={`${size} flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-800 text-sm font-semibold text-white`}>{ini}</span>;
}

// Shows whether an event is still ahead or already over.
export function StatusBadge({ upcoming }: { upcoming: boolean }) {
  return (
    <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${upcoming ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'}`}>
      {upcoming ? 'Upcoming' : 'Previous'}
    </span>
  );
}



===== FILE: components\WaveCard.tsx =====

'use client';

import type { CSSProperties, HTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useInView } from '@/lib/use-in-view';

type Props = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & { children: ReactNode; index?: number };

export default function WaveCard({ children, className = '', index = 0, ...props }: Props) {
  const { ref, isVisible } = useInView<HTMLDivElement>(0.06);
  return (
    <div
      {...props}
      ref={ref}
      data-motion-visible={isVisible}
      style={{ ...props.style, '--reveal-delay': isVisible ? `${Math.min(index, 8) * 90}ms` : '0ms' } as CSSProperties}
      className={`wave-card ${isVisible ? 'is-visible' : ''} ${className}`}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: lib\events.ts =====

export type EventRow = {
  id: number; title: string; venue_date: string | null; description: string | null; image_path: string | null;
  starts_on: string | null; ends_on: string | null; register_url: string | null; is_paid: boolean; fee_amount: number | null;
};

// Today's date in the visitor's own time zone, as YYYY-MM-DD. Call this only inside effects or handlers.
export const localToday = () => new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA');

// An event is upcoming until its last day has passed. An event with no date stays upcoming.
// Until today's date is known, nothing counts as upcoming, so no button or fee flashes up for a past event.
export const isUpcoming = (e: Pick<EventRow, 'starts_on' | 'ends_on'>, today: string) =>
  !!today && (e.ends_on ?? e.starts_on ?? '9999-12-31') >= today;

export const feeLabel = (e: Pick<EventRow, 'is_paid' | 'fee_amount'>) =>
  e.is_paid ? `Fee: UGX ${Number(e.fee_amount ?? 0).toLocaleString('en-US')}` : 'Free';

// Only web links are allowed as registration links.
export const safeUrl = (u: string | null | undefined) => (u && /^https?:\/\//i.test(u) ? u : null);



===== FILE: lib\pesapal.ts =====

// Pesapal API 3.0 client. This module is used only by server-side payment handlers.
function apiBase() {
  const environment = process.env.PESAPAL_ENV ?? 'sandbox';
  if (environment === 'live') return 'https://pay.pesapal.com/v3/api';
  if (environment === 'sandbox') return 'https://cybqa.pesapal.com/pesapalv3/api';
  throw new Error('PESAPAL_ENV must be sandbox or live');
}

let cached: { token: string; exp: number } | null = null;

async function responseJson(response: Response) {
  const text = await response.text();
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    throw new Error(`Pesapal returned an invalid response (${response.status})`);
  }
}

async function token(): Promise<string> {
  if (cached && cached.exp > Date.now() + 30_000) return cached.token;
  const key = process.env.PESAPAL_CONSUMER_KEY;
  const secret = process.env.PESAPAL_CONSUMER_SECRET;
  if (!key || !secret) throw new Error('Pesapal credentials are not configured');

  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase()}/Auth/RequestToken`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ consumer_key: key, consumer_secret: secret }),
  });
  const body = await responseJson(response);
  const expiresAt = Date.parse(body.expiryDate);
  if (!response.ok || typeof body.token !== 'string' || !Number.isFinite(expiresAt)) {
    throw new Error('Pesapal authentication failed');
  }
  cached = { token: body.token, exp: expiresAt };
  return body.token;
}

async function call(path: string, init?: RequestInit) {
  const accessToken = await token();
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase()}/${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Accept: 'application/json',
      Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
    },
  });
  const body = await responseJson(response);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Pesapal request failed (${response.status})`);
  return body;
}

// Run once per environment, then store the returned ipn_id in PESAPAL_IPN_ID.
export const registerIpn = (url: string) =>
  call('URLSetup/RegisterIPN', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ url, ipn_notification_type: 'GET' }) });

export type PesapalOrder = {
  order_tracking_id?: string;
  merchant_reference?: string;
  redirect_url?: string;
};

export type PesapalStatus = {
  merchant_reference?: string;
  status_code?: number;
  amount?: number | string;
  currency?: string;
};

export const submitOrder = async (order: {
  id: string;
  amount: number;
  description: string;
  email: string;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  phone?: string;
}): Promise<PesapalOrder> => {
  const appUrl = process.env.APP_URL;
  const notificationId = process.env.PESAPAL_IPN_ID;
  if (!appUrl || !notificationId) throw new Error('Pesapal callback settings are not configured');

  let callbackUrl: URL;
  try {
    callbackUrl = new URL('/payment/return', appUrl);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('The application URL is invalid');
  }
  if (process.env.PESAPAL_ENV === 'live' && callbackUrl.protocol !== 'https:') {
    throw new Error('Live Pesapal payments require an HTTPS application URL');
  }

  return call('Transactions/SubmitOrderRequest', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({
      id: order.id,
      currency: 'UGX',
      amount: order.amount,
      description: order.description,
      callback_url: callbackUrl.toString(),
      notification_id: notificationId,
      billing_address: {
        email_address: order.email,
        phone_number: order.phone ?? '',
        first_name: order.firstName,
        last_name: order.lastName,
      },
    }),
  });
};

export const transactionStatus = (orderTrackingId: string): Promise<PesapalStatus> =>
  call(`Transactions/GetTransactionStatus?orderTrackingId=${encodeURIComponent(orderTrackingId)}`);



===== FILE: lib\settle.ts =====

import { admin } from './supabase';
import { transactionStatus } from './pesapal';

export type Settled = 'completed' | 'failed' | 'pending' | 'unknown';

// Asks Pesapal for the real status of a payment and activates the membership only if everything matches
// our own record. Used by both the notification (IPN) and the page members land on after paying.
export async function settlePayment(trackingId: string, ref: string): Promise<Settled> {
  const db = admin();
  const { data: pay, error: paymentError } = await db.from('payments').select('*').eq('id', ref).maybeSingle();
  if (paymentError) {
    console.error('Payment lookup failed:', paymentError.message);
    throw new Error('Payment could not be verified');
  }
  if (!pay) return 'unknown';
  if (pay.order_tracking_id && pay.order_tracking_id !== trackingId) return 'unknown';   // reference and tracking id must belong together
  if (pay.status === 'completed') return 'completed';

  const s = await transactionStatus(trackingId);
  if (s.merchant_reference !== pay.id) return 'unknown';

  if (s.status_code === 1 && Number(s.amount) === Number(pay.amount) && s.currency === pay.currency) {
    const { error } = await db.rpc('confirm_payment', { p_id: pay.id, p_tracking: trackingId });
    if (error) {
      console.error('Payment confirmation failed:', error.message);
      return 'pending';
    }
    return 'completed';
  }
  if (s.status_code === 2 || s.status_code === 3) {
    const { error } = await db.from('payments')
      .update({ status: s.status_code === 2 ? 'failed' : 'reversed' })
      .eq('id', pay.id)
      .neq('status', 'completed');
    if (error) {
      console.error('Failed payment status update failed:', error.message);
      return 'pending';
    }
    return 'failed';
  }
  return 'pending';
}



===== FILE: lib\supabase.ts =====

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

// Secret key client: server only, bypasses RLS. Never import this in client code.
export const admin = () =>
  createClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!, { auth: { persistSession: false } });

// Client acting as the signed-in visitor, read from cookies.
export async function userClient() {
  const jar = await cookies();
  return createServerClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!, {
    cookies: {
      getAll: () => jar.getAll(),
      setAll: (list) => {
        try { list.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => jar.set(name, value, options)); } catch { /* called from a page; middleware refreshes instead */ }
      },
    },
  });
}



===== FILE: lib\ui-sound.ts =====

export type UiSound = 'success' | 'delete' | 'attention';

const SOUND_SETTING = 'sru-ui-sounds';
const ATTENTION_SETTING = 'sru-last-attention-sound';
let audioContext: AudioContext | null = null;

function getAudioContext() {
  if (!window.AudioContext) return null;
  if (!audioContext || audioContext.state === 'closed') audioContext = new window.AudioContext();
  return audioContext;
}

export function primeUiSounds() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || window.localStorage.getItem(SOUND_SETTING) === 'off') return;
  const context = getAudioContext();
  if (context?.state === 'suspended') {
    void context.resume().catch(error => console.debug('UI audio could not be enabled:', error));
  }
}

function playTone(context: AudioContext, sound: UiSound) {
  const oscillator = context.createOscillator();
  const gain = context.createGain();
  const now = context.currentTime;
  const frequency = sound === 'delete' ? 330 : sound === 'attention' ? 660 : 520;

  oscillator.type = 'sine';
  oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);
  if (sound === 'attention') oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, now + 0.1);
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.07, now + 0.015);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + (sound === 'attention' ? 0.2 : 0.12));
  oscillator.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(context.destination);
  oscillator.start(now);
  oscillator.stop(now + (sound === 'attention' ? 0.21 : 0.13));
}

export function playUiSound(sound: UiSound) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || window.localStorage.getItem(SOUND_SETTING) === 'off') return;

  if (sound === 'attention') {
    const lastPlayed = Number(window.localStorage.getItem(ATTENTION_SETTING) ?? 0);
    if (Date.now() - lastPlayed < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) return;
    window.localStorage.setItem(ATTENTION_SETTING, String(Date.now()));
  }

  const context = getAudioContext();
  if (!context) return;
  if (context.state === 'suspended') {
    void context.resume()
      .then(() => playTone(context, sound))
      .catch(error => console.debug('UI sound could not be played:', error));
    return;
  }
  playTone(context, sound);
}



===== FILE: lib\use-in-view.ts =====

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

export function useInView<T extends HTMLElement>(threshold = 0.08) {
  const ref = useRef<T>(null);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = ref.current;
    if (!element) return;

    if (window.location.pathname === '/admin' || window.location.pathname.startsWith('/admin/')) {
      setIsVisible(true);
      return;
    }

    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window) || window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
      setIsVisible(true);
      return;
    }

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      setIsVisible(entry.isIntersecting);
    }, { threshold, rootMargin: '0px 0px -6% 0px' });

    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [threshold]);

  return { ref, isVisible };
}

===== FILE: proxy.ts =====

import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr';

// Keeps the member's sign-in session fresh on every page visit.
// (Next.js 16 calls this file "proxy"; older versions called it "middleware".)
export async function proxy(req: NextRequest) {
  let res = NextResponse.next({ request: req });
  const sb = createServerClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!, {
    cookies: {
      getAll: () => req.cookies.getAll(),
      setAll: (list) => {
        list.forEach(({ name, value }) => req.cookies.set(name, value));
        res = NextResponse.next({ request: req });
        list.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => res.cookies.set(name, value, options));
      },
    },
  });
  await sb.auth.getUser();
  return res;
}

export const config = { matcher: ['/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|api/pay/ipn).*)'] };



===== FILE: README.md =====

# SRU membership portal: backend files

Merge these into a fresh Next.js 16 project (App Router, no src folder, "@/*" alias).
See the chat guide for the exact commands.

- supabase/schema.sql   run once in Supabase SQL Editor
- supabase/patch-1.sql  run once, after schema.sql (security fix and storage rules)
- supabase/patch-7-article-decline-reason.sql after patch 4 (adds the article moderation reason column)
- supabase/patch-8-article-engagement.sql after patch 7 (enables safe member comments, edits, likes and unlikes)
- supabase/patch-6-application-based-registration.sql after patches 1-5 (registration stays in applications until payment)
- supabase/patch-9-professional-join-fields-and-stats.sql after patch 6 (professional join details and designation aggregates)
- supabase/patch-10-committee-message.sql after the committee terms/content patches (committee messages and public committee data)
- supabase/patch-11-homepage-showcases.sql after patch-4-admin-content-crud.sql (admin-managed homepage showcase segments)
- supabase/patch-12-showcase-ad-labels.sql after patch 11 (optional showcase sub-labels)
- supabase/patch-13-public-committee-directory.sql after patch-10-committee-message.sql (public full committee history)
- .env.example          copy to .env.local and fill in. Never share or upload .env.local
- proxy.ts              keeps sign-in sessions fresh (called middleware.ts before Next.js 16)
- lib/                  Pesapal client, Supabase clients, payment confirmation
- app/api/pay           start a payment, receive Pesapal notifications
- app/join, app/payment/return, app/auth/callback   registration, return page, Google redirect
- components/GoogleOneTap.tsx   one-tap sign-in

## Production payments and deployment

The payment flow uses Pesapal. A registration stays in `applications` while payment is pending.
Only after the server verifies the transaction does `confirm_payment()` create the `members` row
and set its paid-through year.

Before enabling live payments:

1. Deploy the app with an HTTPS `APP_URL`.
2. Set the environment variables from `.env.example` in the Vercel project settings. Keep
   `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` and both Pesapal consumer credentials server-only; never use
   `NEXT_PUBLIC_` for them or commit them to source control.
3. Register `https://<your-domain>/api/pay/ipn` with Pesapal using GET notifications, then set
   its returned ID as `PESAPAL_IPN_ID`.
4. Add the deployed `/auth/callback` URL to the Supabase Auth redirect allowlist and configure
   the Google provider's authorized redirect URI in Supabase/Google.
5. Test registration, renewal, cancellation, delayed notifications, and payment confirmation
   with Pesapal sandbox credentials before setting `PESAPAL_ENV=live` and using production
   credentials.

Do not mark a member active manually or rely on the browser return URL alone. The server verifies
the merchant reference, amount, currency, and provider status before activating membership.

  using (author_id = auth.uid())
  with check (author_id = auth.uid() and status = 'pending');

-- Members can edit or delete their own CPD and career entries while pending or rejected. Verified entries are locked.
-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor.
grant delete on cpd_entries, career_entries to authenticated;

create policy "cpd edit own" on cpd_entries for update to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending','rejected') and not added_by_staff)
  with check (member_id = auth.uid() and status = 'pending' and not added_by_staff);
create policy "cpd delete own" on cpd_entries for delete to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending','rejected') and not added_by_staff);

create policy "career edit own" on career_entries for update to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending','rejected'))
  with check (member_id = auth.uid() and status = 'pending');
create policy "career delete own" on career_entries for delete to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending','rejected'));

-- Members can delete their own old files when they replace or remove them.
create policy "proofs delete own" on storage.objects for delete to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'proofs' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy "media delete own" on storage.objects for delete to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'public-media' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);



===== FILE: supabase\patch-1.sql =====

-- Run this ONCE in the Supabase SQL Editor, after schema.sql.

-- 1. Members are created by the verified-payment function, never by the browser.
drop policy if exists "member self insert" on public.members;
revoke insert on public.members from anon, authenticated;

-- 2. Storage rules. Members upload into a folder named after their own user id.
-- proofs (private): certificates, admission letters, posting letters. Staff can read them for verification.
create policy "proofs upload own" on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (bucket_id = 'proofs' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy "proofs read" on storage.objects for select to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'proofs' and ((storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text or public.is_staff()));

-- public-media (public): photos, logo, hero images. Members use their own folder, admins can write anywhere.
create policy "media upload" on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (bucket_id = 'public-media' and ((storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text or public.is_admin()));
create policy "media replace admin" on storage.objects for update to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'public-media' and public.is_admin());



===== FILE: supabase\patch-10-committee-message.sql =====

-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor after the committee terms/content patches.
-- Adds a public message for each committee member while keeping contact details private.

begin;

alter table public.committee
  add column if not exists message text;

grant select, insert, update, delete on public.committee to authenticated;

drop function if exists public.public_committee();

create function public.public_committee()
returns table (
  term_id bigint,
  starts_on date,
  ends_on date,
  is_current boolean,
  role text,
  full_name text,
  photo_path text,
  message text
)
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  with recent_terms as (
    select id, starts_on, ends_on, row_number() over (order by starts_on desc, id desc) as position
    from public.committee_terms
  )
  select
    terms.id,
    terms.starts_on,
    terms.ends_on,
    terms.ends_on is null,
    members.role,
    members.full_name,
    members.photo_path,
    members.message
  from recent_terms as terms
  join public.committee as members on members.term_id = terms.id
  where terms.position <= 2
  order by terms.starts_on desc, members.sort, members.id
$$;

revoke execute on function public.public_committee() from public;
grant execute on function public.public_committee() to anon, authenticated;

commit;



===== FILE: supabase\patch-11-homepage-showcases.sql =====

-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor after schema.sql and patch-4-admin-content-crud.sql.
-- Adds three admin-managed image/message placements for the public homepage.

create table if not exists public.homepage_showcases (
  slot smallint primary key check (slot between 1 and 3),
  label text,
  sub_label text,
  message text not null default '',
  image_path text,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.homepage_showcases enable row level security;

grant select on public.homepage_showcases to anon, authenticated;
grant insert, update, delete on public.homepage_showcases to authenticated;

drop policy if exists "homepage showcases public read" on public.homepage_showcases;
create policy "homepage showcases public read" on public.homepage_showcases
  for select to anon, authenticated
  using (image_path is not null and length(trim(message)) > 0);

drop policy if exists "homepage showcases admin read" on public.homepage_showcases;
create policy "homepage showcases admin read" on public.homepage_showcases
  for select to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "homepage showcases admin insert" on public.homepage_showcases;
create policy "homepage showcases admin insert" on public.homepage_showcases
  for insert to authenticated
  with check (public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "homepage showcases admin update" on public.homepage_showcases;
create policy "homepage showcases admin update" on public.homepage_showcases
  for update to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin())
  with check (public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "homepage showcases admin delete" on public.homepage_showcases;
create policy "homepage showcases admin delete" on public.homepage_showcases
  for delete to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin());

===== FILE: supabase\patch-13-public-committee-directory.sql =====

-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor after patch-10-committee-message.sql.
-- Exposes the complete committee history without exposing private contact fields.

create or replace function public.public_all_committees()
returns table (
  member_id bigint,
  term_id bigint,
  starts_on date,
  ends_on date,
  is_current boolean,
  role text,
  full_name text,
  photo_path text,
  message text
)
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select
    members.id,
    terms.id,
    terms.starts_on,
    terms.ends_on,
    terms.ends_on is null,
    members.role,
    members.full_name,
    members.photo_path,
    members.message
  from public.committee_terms as terms
  left join public.committee as members on members.term_id = terms.id
  order by terms.starts_on desc, terms.id desc, members.sort, members.id
$$;

revoke execute on function public.public_all_committees() from public;
grant execute on function public.public_all_committees() to anon, authenticated;

notify pgrst, 'reload schema';



===== FILE: supabase\patch-2.sql =====

-- Run ONCE in the Supabase SQL Editor (after schema.sql and patch-1.sql).
-- Lets staff see member names in the CPD review queue without opening the whole members table to coordinators.

create function cpd_queue() returns table (
  id uuid, member_name text, member_type text, title text, points int, year int,
  proof_path text, status review_status, rejection_reason text, added_by_staff boolean
) language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select c.id, m.full_name, m.member_type, c.title, c.points, c.year, c.proof_path, c.status, c.rejection_reason, c.added_by_staff
  from cpd_entries c join members m on m.id = c.member_id
  where is_staff()
  order by (c.status = 'pending') desc, c.created_at desc $$;

create function member_directory() returns table (id uuid, full_name text, member_type text, paid_through_year int)
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select id, full_name, member_type, paid_through_year from members where is_staff() order by full_name $$;

revoke execute on function cpd_queue() from public, anon;
revoke execute on function member_directory() from public, anon;
grant execute on function cpd_queue() to authenticated;
grant execute on function member_directory() to authenticated;



===== FILE: supabase\patch-3-member-entry-crud.sql =====

-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor after schema.sql, patch-1.sql and patch-2.sql.
-- Members may update or delete their own pending/rejected submissions. Verified and
-- staff-awarded CPD records remain protected by both RLS and the trigger guards below.

drop policy if exists "cpd member edit" on public.cpd_entries;
create policy "cpd member edit" on public.cpd_entries
  for update to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'rejected') and not added_by_staff)
  with check (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'rejected') and not added_by_staff);

drop policy if exists "cpd member delete" on public.cpd_entries;
create policy "cpd member delete" on public.cpd_entries
  for delete to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'rejected') and not added_by_staff);

drop policy if exists "career member edit" on public.career_entries;
create policy "career member edit" on public.career_entries
  for update to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'rejected'))
  with check (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'rejected'));

drop policy if exists "career member delete" on public.career_entries;
create policy "career member delete" on public.career_entries
  for delete to authenticated
  using (member_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'rejected'));

grant delete on public.cpd_entries, public.career_entries to authenticated;

create or replace function public.protect_member_cpd_edits()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
set search_path = public, auth
as $$
begin
  if old.member_id = auth.uid() and not public.is_staff() then
    if old.status = 'verified' or old.added_by_staff then
      raise exception 'Members cannot edit verified or staff-awarded CPD entries';
    end if;
    if new.member_id is distinct from old.member_id
      or new.year is distinct from old.year
      or new.added_by_staff is distinct from old.added_by_staff
      or new.created_at is distinct from old.created_at then
      raise exception 'Members can only edit the details and proof of their CPD entry';
    end if;
    if old.status = 'pending' then
      if new.status is distinct from old.status
        or new.rejection_reason is distinct from old.rejection_reason
        or new.reviewed_by is distinct from old.reviewed_by then
        raise exception 'Pending CPD review fields cannot be changed by a member';
      end if;
    elsif old.status = 'rejected' then
      if new.status <> 'pending'
        or new.rejection_reason is not null
        or new.reviewed_by is not null then
        raise exception 'Editing a rejected CPD entry must resubmit it for review';
      end if;
    end if;
    if new.proof_path is not null
      and (storage.foldername(new.proof_path))[1] <> auth.uid()::text then
      raise exception 'CPD proof must be stored in the memberâ€™s own folder';
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;

drop trigger if exists protect_member_cpd_edits on public.cpd_entries;
create trigger protect_member_cpd_edits
  before update on public.cpd_entries
  for each row execute function public.protect_member_cpd_edits();

create or replace function public.protect_member_career_edits()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
set search_path = public, auth
as $$
begin
  if old.member_id = auth.uid() and not public.is_staff() then
    if old.status = 'verified' then
      raise exception 'Members cannot edit verified career entries';
    end if;
    if new.member_id is distinct from old.member_id then
      raise exception 'Members can only edit the details and proof of their career entry';
    end if;
    if old.status = 'pending' then
      if new.status is distinct from old.status
        or new.rejection_reason is distinct from old.rejection_reason
        or new.reviewed_by is distinct from old.reviewed_by then
        raise exception 'Pending career review fields cannot be changed by a member';
      end if;
    elsif old.status = 'rejected' then
      if new.status <> 'pending'
        or new.rejection_reason is not null
        or new.reviewed_by is not null then
        raise exception 'Editing a rejected career entry must resubmit it for review';
      end if;
    end if;
    if new.proof_path is null
      or (storage.foldername(new.proof_path))[1] <> auth.uid()::text then
      raise exception 'Career proof must be stored in the memberâ€™s own folder';
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;

drop trigger if exists protect_member_career_edits on public.career_entries;
create trigger protect_member_career_edits
  before update on public.career_entries
  for each row execute function public.protect_member_career_edits();

drop policy if exists "proofs delete own unverified" on storage.objects;
create policy "proofs delete own unverified" on storage.objects
  for delete to authenticated
  using (
    bucket_id = 'proofs'
    and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text
    and not exists (
      select 1 from public.cpd_entries c
      where c.proof_path = objects.name and c.status = 'verified'
    )
    and not exists (
      select 1 from public.career_entries c
      where c.proof_path = objects.name and c.status = 'verified'
    )
  );



===== FILE: supabase\patch-4-admin-content-crud.sql =====

-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor after schema.sql and the earlier patches.
-- Adds admin editing for member details and committee terms, and allows admins
-- to remove unused files from the public-media bucket.

grant update (full_name, member_type, reg_no, phone, workplace, bio)
  on public.members to authenticated;

drop policy if exists "members admin edit" on public.members;
create policy "members admin edit" on public.members
  for update to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin())
  with check (public.is_admin());

grant select, update, delete on public.committee_terms to authenticated;

drop policy if exists "committee terms admin read" on public.committee_terms;
create policy "committee terms admin read" on public.committee_terms
  for select to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "committee terms admin edit" on public.committee_terms;
create policy "committee terms admin edit" on public.committee_terms
  for update to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin())
  with check (public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "committee terms admin delete" on public.committee_terms;
create policy "committee terms admin delete" on public.committee_terms
  for delete to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin());

grant update (title, body, image_path, status, decline_reason) on public.articles to authenticated;
grant insert (author_id, title, body, image_path, status) on public.articles to authenticated;
grant delete on public.articles to authenticated;

drop policy if exists "articles author edit" on public.articles;
create policy "articles author edit" on public.articles
  for update to authenticated
  using (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'declined'))
  with check (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'declined'));

  for delete to authenticated
  using (author_id = auth.uid() and status in ('pending', 'declined'));

create or replace function public.protect_member_article_edits()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
set search_path = public, auth
as $$
begin
  if old.author_id = auth.uid() and not public.is_admin() then
    if old.status = 'approved' then
      raise exception 'Members cannot edit published articles';
    end if;
    if new.author_id is distinct from old.author_id
      or new.created_at is distinct from old.created_at then
      raise exception 'Members can only edit their own article content';
    end if;
    if old.status = 'pending' and new.status is distinct from old.status then
      raise exception 'Pending articles cannot change review status';
    end if;
    if old.status = 'declined' and new.status <> 'pending' then
      raise exception 'Editing a declined article must resubmit it for approval';
    end if;
    if new.image_path is not null
      and (storage.foldername(new.image_path))[1] <> auth.uid()::text then
      raise exception 'Article images must be stored in the memberâ€™s own folder';
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;

drop trigger if exists protect_member_article_edits on public.articles;
create trigger protect_member_article_edits
  before update on public.articles
  for each row execute function public.protect_member_article_edits();

drop policy if exists "media delete admin" on storage.objects;
create policy "media delete admin" on storage.objects
  for delete to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'public-media' and public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "media delete own article image" on storage.objects;
create policy "media delete own article image" on storage.objects
  for delete to authenticated
  using (
    bucket_id = 'public-media'
    and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text
    and not exists (
      select 1 from public.articles a
      where a.image_path = objects.name
    )
  );



===== FILE: supabase\patch-5-staff-record-crud.sql =====

-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor after schema.sql and patches 1-4.
-- Staff can correct CPD records; admins can correct career records.

grant update (title, points, year, proof_path, status, rejection_reason, reviewed_by)
  on public.cpd_entries to authenticated;
grant delete on public.cpd_entries to authenticated;

drop policy if exists "cpd staff delete" on public.cpd_entries;
create policy "cpd staff delete" on public.cpd_entries
  for delete to authenticated
  using (public.my_role() in ('chief_admin', 'admin', 'cpd_coordinator'));

grant update (kind, organisation, title, from_year, to_year, proof_path, status, rejection_reason, reviewed_by)
  on public.career_entries to authenticated;
grant delete on public.career_entries to authenticated;

  for delete to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin());

create policy "proofs upload staff" on storage.objects
  for insert to authenticated
  with check (bucket_id = 'proofs' and public.is_staff());

create or replace function public.proof_is_unreferenced(p_path text)
returns boolean
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select public.is_staff()
    and not exists (
    select 1 from public.cpd_entries c where c.proof_path = p_path
  ) and not exists (
    select 1 from public.career_entries c where c.proof_path = p_path
  );
$$;

revoke all on function public.proof_is_unreferenced(text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.proof_is_unreferenced(text) to authenticated;

drop policy if exists "proofs delete staff unreferenced" on storage.objects;
create policy "proofs delete staff unreferenced" on storage.objects
  for delete to authenticated
  using (
    bucket_id = 'proofs'
    and public.is_staff()
    and public.proof_is_unreferenced(name)
  );

drop function if exists public.cpd_queue();
create function public.cpd_queue() returns table (
  id uuid, member_id uuid, member_name text, member_type text, title text, points int, year int,
  proof_path text, status review_status, rejection_reason text, added_by_staff boolean
) language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select c.id, c.member_id, m.full_name, m.member_type, c.title, c.points, c.year,
    c.proof_path, c.status, c.rejection_reason, c.added_by_staff
  from public.cpd_entries c join public.members m on m.id = c.member_id
  where public.is_staff()
  order by (c.status = 'pending') desc, c.created_at desc
$$;

revoke execute on function public.cpd_queue() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.cpd_queue() to authenticated;



===== FILE: supabase\patch-6-application-based-registration.sql =====

-- Run once in the Supabase SQL Editor after schema.sql and patches 1-5.
-- Applicants remain in applications until a verified registration payment is confirmed.

begin;

create table if not exists public.applications (
  id uuid primary key references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  email text not null,
  full_name text not null,
  member_type text not null references public.membership_types(code),
  reg_no text,
  phone text,
  workplace text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.applications enable row level security;
drop policy if exists "application self read" on public.applications;
drop policy if exists "application self insert" on public.applications;
drop policy if exists "application self update" on public.applications;
create policy "application self read" on public.applications
  for select to authenticated using (id = auth.uid());
create policy "application self insert" on public.applications
  for insert to authenticated
  with check (id = auth.uid() and email = auth.jwt()->>'email');
create policy "application self update" on public.applications
  for update to authenticated
  using (id = auth.uid())
  with check (id = auth.uid() and email = auth.jwt()->>'email');
grant select, insert, update on public.applications to authenticated;

insert into public.applications (id, email, full_name, member_type, reg_no, phone, workplace)
  select id, email, full_name, member_type, reg_no, phone, workplace
  from public.members
  where paid_through_year is null
on conflict (id) do nothing;

alter table public.payments alter column member_id drop not null;
alter table public.payments add column if not exists applicant_id uuid;
update public.payments as payment
  set applicant_id = payment.member_id, member_id = null
  from public.members as member
  where payment.member_id = member.id
    and member.paid_through_year is null
    and payment.purpose = 'registration'
    and payment.status <> 'completed';
drop policy if exists "own payments" on public.payments;
create policy "own payments" on public.payments
  for select using (member_id = auth.uid() or applicant_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "member self insert" on public.members;
revoke insert on public.members from anon, authenticated;

create or replace function public.confirm_payment(p_id uuid, p_tracking text)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  p public.payments;
  a public.applications;
  target_member_id uuid;
begin
  select * into p from public.payments where id = p_id for update;
  if not found or p.status = 'completed' then return; end if;

  if p.purpose = 'registration' then
    target_member_id := coalesce(p.applicant_id, p.member_id);
    if target_member_id is null then raise exception 'Registration payment has no applicant'; end if;
    select * into a from public.applications where id = target_member_id;
    if not found then raise exception 'Registration application was not found'; end if;

    insert into public.members as existing_member (id, email, full_name, member_type, reg_no, phone, workplace, paid_through_year)
      values (a.id, a.email, a.full_name, a.member_type, a.reg_no, a.phone, a.workplace, p.year)
      on conflict (id) do update set
        email = excluded.email,
        full_name = excluded.full_name,
        member_type = excluded.member_type,
        reg_no = excluded.reg_no,
        phone = excluded.phone,
        workplace = excluded.workplace,
        paid_through_year = greatest(coalesce(existing_member.paid_through_year, 0), excluded.paid_through_year);
  else
    target_member_id := p.member_id;
    if target_member_id is null then raise exception 'Renewal payment has no member'; end if;
    update public.members
      set paid_through_year = greatest(coalesce(paid_through_year, 0), p.year)
      where id = target_member_id;
    if not found then raise exception 'Member for renewal payment was not found'; end if;
  end if;

  update public.payments set member_id = target_member_id, status = 'completed',
    confirmed_at = now(), order_tracking_id = p_tracking where id = p_id;
end;
$$;

revoke execute on function public.confirm_payment(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.confirm_payment(uuid, text) to service_role;

create or replace function public.member_directory()
returns table (id uuid, full_name text, member_type text, paid_through_year int)
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select id, full_name, member_type, paid_through_year
  from public.members
  where public.is_staff() and paid_through_year is not null
  order by full_name
$$;
revoke execute on function public.member_directory() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.member_directory() to authenticated;

commit;
